# MoonBit's TLSF allocator

What package `tlsf` defines and proves, in order, with the kernel's statement of each (every lemma is a theorem: loading the package proves it). Generated by `tlsf/tlsf_test.mbt`; run `moon test --update` to refresh it.

Types are abbreviated: `Byte`, `W32`, `W64` are words of 8, 32, 64 bits; `Heap` is `(Num) -> Option[Byte]` (what each address holds); `HProp` is a heap assertion `(Heap) -> Bool`; `Mem` is a WasmCert memory `(Limit_t, List[Byte])`; `Assn` is `(Wstore, Wframe, List[Value]) -> Bool` (an assertion on store, frame and value stack); `SAssn` is `(List[Value], List[Value], List[Value], Num, Heap) -> Bool` (on the value stack, the locals, the globals, the memory's length in bytes and the memory as a heap); `GProp` is `(List[Value], Num, Heap) -> Bool` (on the globals, the memory's length and the heap, the `R` of an exact state `st(v, l, R)`); `FProp` is `(List[Value], List[Value], Num, Heap) -> Bool` (on a list of values, the globals, the memory's length and the heap: a function's precondition or postcondition, on its arguments or results, or a loop's invariant, on the locals); `Specs` is `(Num, FProp, FProp) -> Bool` (a set of function specs: which function, its precondition and its postcondition); `Tabs` is `List[(Tab_t, List[V_ref])]` (a store's tables, each its type and its entries).

A program (`program`) is a constant defined as a list of instructions; its entry shows the list in WebAssembly's text format, one instruction per line, printed from the kernel's definition and checked to read back as it (wasm/wat.mbt). An instruction it has no name for is written in the term syntax after `hol`.

A statement is laid out as `∀ (x y : T) (z : U)`, its variables grouped by type; then, if it has hypotheses, `if` and the numbered hypotheses H1, ..., Hn and `then` and the conclusion C, with `or` between alternatives and `&&` before each further conjunct (as between a definition's equations). It stands for `∀ x y z, H1 && ... && Hn → C`. A line longer than 80 characters is broken by its brackets and operators, an argument, conjunct or case per line; only whitespace changes. Each layout is printed from the kernel's statement and checked to read back as it (wasm/layout.mbt).

## TLSF size classes (tlsf.mbt)

definition `tlsf_fli`

```
tlsf_fli((s : W32)) == word_sub(word(31), int_clz(s))
```

definition `tlsf_sli`

```
tlsf_sli((s : W32), (f : W32))
  == word_and(word_jushr(s, word_sub(f, word(4))), word(15))
```

definition `tlsf_mask`

```
tlsf_mask((s : W32))
  == word_sub(word_jshl(word(1), word_sub(tlsf_fli(s), word(4))), word(1))
```

definition `tlsf_round`

```
tlsf_round((s : W32))
  == word_and(word_add(s, tlsf_mask(s)), word_not(tlsf_mask(s)))
```

lemma `tlsf_exp_le`

```
∀ (m n : Num), (EXP(2, m) <= EXP(2, n)) == (m <= n)
```

lemma `tlsf_exp_lt`

```
∀ (m n : Num), (EXP(2, m) < EXP(2, n)) == (m < n)
```

lemma `tlsf_exp_nz`

```
∀ (n : Num), !(EXP(2, n) == 0)
```

lemma `tlsf_exp_fli`

```
∀ (f : Num)
if
  1. 4 <= f
then
  EXP(2, f) == 16 * EXP(2, f - 4)
  && EXP(2, f + 1) == 32 * EXP(2, f - 4)
  && EXP(2, f + 2) == 64 * EXP(2, f - 4)
  && EXP(2, f + 1 - 4) == 2 * EXP(2, f - 4)
```

lemma `tlsf_fli_clz`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. !(s == word(0))
then
  val(tlsf_fli(s)) == 31 - word_clz(s)
```

lemma `tlsf_fli_bounds`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. !(s == word(0))
then
  val(tlsf_fli(s)) <= 31
  && EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s))) <= val(s)
  && val(s) < EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) + 1)
```

lemma `tlsf_fli_val`

```
∀ (s : W32) (f : Num)
if
  1. EXP(2, f) <= val(s)
  2. val(s) < EXP(2, f + 1)
then
  val(tlsf_fli(s)) == f
```

lemma `tlsf_sli_val`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
then
  4 <= val(tlsf_fli(s))
  && val(tlsf_fli(s)) <= 31
  && val(tlsf_sli(s, tlsf_fli(s)))
       == MOD(DIV(val(s), EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4)), 16)
```

lemma `tlsf_class_num`

```
∀ (v k : Num)
if
  1. EXP(2, k + 4) <= v
  2. v < EXP(2, k + 5)
then
  16 <= DIV(v, EXP(2, k))
  && DIV(v, EXP(2, k)) < 32
  && MOD(DIV(v, EXP(2, k)), 16) == DIV(v, EXP(2, k)) - 16
  && DIV(v, EXP(2, k)) * EXP(2, k) <= v
  && v < DIV(v, EXP(2, k)) * EXP(2, k) + EXP(2, k)
```

lemma `tlsf_class`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
then
  4 <= val(tlsf_fli(s))
  && val(tlsf_fli(s)) <= 31
  && val(tlsf_sli(s, tlsf_fli(s))) < 16
  && (16 + val(tlsf_sli(s, tlsf_fli(s)))) * EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4) <= val(
       s)
  && val(s) < (17 + val(tlsf_sli(s, tlsf_fli(s)))) * EXP(
       2,
       val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4)
```

lemma `tlsf_mask_word`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
then
  tlsf_mask(s) == word(EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4) - 1)
```

lemma `tlsf_round_val`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(tlsf_round(s)) == EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4) * DIV(
    val(s) + (EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4) - 1),
    EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4))
```

lemma `tlsf_class_exact`

```
∀ (x : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(x)
  2. MOD(val(x), EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(x)) - 4)) == 0
then
  (16 + val(tlsf_sli(x, tlsf_fli(x)))) * EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(x)) - 4) == val(x)
```

lemma `tlsf_round_bounds`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(s) <= val(tlsf_round(s))
  && val(tlsf_round(s)) < val(s) + EXP(2, val(tlsf_fli(s)) - 4)
  && (16 + val(tlsf_sli(tlsf_round(s), tlsf_fli(tlsf_round(s))))) * EXP(
       2,
       val(tlsf_fli(tlsf_round(s))) - 4)
       == val(tlsf_round(s))
```

lemma `tlsf_find_sound`

```
∀ (s t : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. 16 <= val(t)
  4. val(tlsf_fli(tlsf_round(s))) < val(tlsf_fli(t))
     || val(tlsf_fli(t)) == val(tlsf_fli(tlsf_round(s)))
        && val(tlsf_sli(tlsf_round(s), tlsf_fli(tlsf_round(s)))) <= val(
             tlsf_sli(t, tlsf_fli(t)))
then
  val(s) <= val(t)
```

lemma `tlsf_class_mono`

```
∀ (x y : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(x)
  2. val(x) <= val(y)
then
  val(tlsf_fli(x)) < val(tlsf_fli(y))
or
  val(tlsf_fli(y)) == val(tlsf_fli(x))
  && val(tlsf_sli(x, tlsf_fli(x))) <= val(tlsf_sli(y, tlsf_fli(y)))
```

## TLSF free lists: push and pop (tlsf.mbt)

lemma `tlsf_code_fli`

```
∀ (x : W32),
  word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(x))) == word_sub(tlsf_fli(x), word(4))
```

lemma `tlsf_code_sli`

```
∀ (x : W32),
  word_and(word_jushr(x, word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(x)))), word(15))
    == tlsf_sli(x, tlsf_fli(x))
```

program `tlsf_push_code`

```
tlsf_push_code((d : W32)) ==
  local.get 0
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 4294967292
  i32.and
  local.tee 2
  i32.const 27
  local.get 2
  i32.clz
  i32.sub
  local.tee 1
  i32.shr_u
  i32.const 15
  i32.and
  local.set 2
  i32.const d
  i32.const 15
  i32.add
  i32.const 4294967280
  i32.and
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 1
  local.get 1
  i32.shl
  i32.or
  local.set 3
  i32.const d
  i32.const 15
  i32.add
  i32.const 4294967280
  i32.and
  local.get 3
  i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const d
  i32.const 15
  i32.add
  i32.const 4294967280
  i32.and
  i32.const 4
  i32.add
  local.get 1
  i32.const 68
  i32.mul
  i32.add
  local.tee 1
  local.get 1
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 1
  local.get 2
  i32.shl
  i32.or
  i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  local.get 0
  i32.const 12
  i32.add
  local.get 1
  i32.const 4
  i32.add
  local.get 2
  i32.const 2
  i32.shl
  i32.add
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  local.tee 3
  i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  local.get 3
  if
    local.get 3
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    local.get 0
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  end
  local.get 1
  i32.const 4
  i32.add
  local.get 2
  i32.const 2
  i32.shl
  i32.add
  local.get 0
  i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  local.get 0
  if
    local.get 0
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    i32.const 0
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  end
  i32.const 0
```

program `tlsf_pop_code`

```
tlsf_pop_code((d : W32)) ==
  local.get 0
  i32.const 8
  i32.add
  local.tee 1
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  if (result i32)
    local.get 1
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    local.tee 1
    i32.const 12
    i32.add
    local.get 0
    i32.const 12
    i32.add
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    local.tee 0
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 0
    if
      local.get 0
      i32.const 8
      i32.add
      local.get 1
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    end
    i32.const 0
  else
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 4294967292
    i32.and
    local.tee 1
    i32.const 27
    local.get 1
    i32.clz
    i32.sub
    local.tee 1
    i32.shr_u
    i32.const 15
    i32.and
    local.set 2
    i32.const d
    i32.const 15
    i32.add
    i32.const 4294967280
    i32.and
    i32.const 4
    i32.add
    local.get 1
    i32.const 68
    i32.mul
    i32.add
    local.tee 3
    i32.const 4
    i32.add
    local.get 2
    i32.const 2
    i32.shl
    i32.add
    local.get 0
    i32.const 12
    i32.add
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    local.tee 4
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 4
    if
      local.get 4
      i32.const 8
      i32.add
      i32.const 0
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    end
    local.get 0
    i32.const 12
    i32.add
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.eqz
    if
      local.get 3
      local.get 3
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 1
      local.get 2
      i32.shl
      i32.xor
      local.tee 0
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
      local.get 0
      i32.eqz
      if
        i32.const d
        i32.const 15
        i32.add
        i32.const 4294967280
        i32.and
        i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
        i32.const 1
        local.get 1
        i32.shl
        i32.xor
        local.set 0
        i32.const d
        i32.const 15
        i32.add
        i32.const 4294967280
        i32.and
        local.get 0
        i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
      end
    end
    i32.const 0
  end
  drop
```

example `tlsf_push_cons`

```
∀ (d m hd pf0 nf0 fl slb nx npf : W32) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
  2. !(nx == word(0))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
                  serialise_i32(fl)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(4)),
                        word_mul(
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                          word(68)))),
                    serialise_i32(slb)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              word_add(
                                word_and(
                                  word_add(d, word(15)),
                                  word(4294967280)),
                                word(4)),
                              word_mul(
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292))))),
                                word(68))),
                            word(4)),
                          word_jshl(
                            word_and(
                              word_jushr(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292)))))),
                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(nx)),
                    star(
                      pts(
                        val(word_add(nx, word(8))),
                        serialise_i32(npf)),
                      fp(gs, ms))))))),
          h)),
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [
        V_num(ConstInt32(m)),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_add(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(4)),
              word_mul(
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                word(68))))),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_and(
              word_jushr(
                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))))),
              word(15)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(nx))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(word(0))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nx)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
                  serialise_i32(
                    word_or(
                      fl,
                      word_jshl(
                        word(1),
                        word_sub(
                          word(27),
                          word(
                            word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))))))),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(4)),
                        word_mul(
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                          word(68)))),
                    serialise_i32(
                      word_or(
                        slb,
                        word_jshl(
                          word(1),
                          word_and(
                            word_jushr(
                              word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                              word_sub(
                                word(27),
                                word(
                                  word_clz(
                                    word_and(
                                      hd,
                                      word(4294967292)))))),
                            word(15)))))),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              word_add(
                                word_and(
                                  word_add(d, word(15)),
                                  word(4294967280)),
                                word(4)),
                              word_mul(
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292))))),
                                word(68))),
                            word(4)),
                          word_jshl(
                            word_and(
                              word_jushr(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292)))))),
                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    star(
                      pts(val(word_add(nx, word(8))), serialise_i32(m)),
                      fp(gs, ms))))))),
          h)))
```

example `tlsf_push_nil`

```
∀ (d m hd pf0 nf0 fl slb nx npf : W32) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
  2. nx == word(0)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
                  serialise_i32(fl)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(4)),
                        word_mul(
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                          word(68)))),
                    serialise_i32(slb)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              word_add(
                                word_and(
                                  word_add(d, word(15)),
                                  word(4294967280)),
                                word(4)),
                              word_mul(
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292))))),
                                word(68))),
                            word(4)),
                          word_jshl(
                            word_and(
                              word_jushr(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292)))))),
                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(nx)),
                    fp(gs, ms)))))),
          h)),
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [
        V_num(ConstInt32(m)),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_add(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(4)),
              word_mul(
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                word(68))))),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_and(
              word_jushr(
                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))))),
              word(15)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(nx))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(word(0))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nx)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
                  serialise_i32(
                    word_or(
                      fl,
                      word_jshl(
                        word(1),
                        word_sub(
                          word(27),
                          word(
                            word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))))))),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(4)),
                        word_mul(
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                          word(68)))),
                    serialise_i32(
                      word_or(
                        slb,
                        word_jshl(
                          word(1),
                          word_and(
                            word_jushr(
                              word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                              word_sub(
                                word(27),
                                word(
                                  word_clz(
                                    word_and(
                                      hd,
                                      word(4294967292)))))),
                            word(15)))))),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              word_add(
                                word_and(
                                  word_add(d, word(15)),
                                  word(4294967280)),
                                word(4)),
                              word_mul(
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292))))),
                                word(68))),
                            word(4)),
                          word_jshl(
                            word_and(
                              word_jushr(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292)))))),
                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    fp(gs, ms)))))),
          h)))
```

            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf)),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf)),
              star(

                  val(

                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_and(
                              word_add(d, word(15)),
                              word(4294967280)),

                          word_mul(

                              word(27),
                              word(

                            word(68))),

                      word_jshl(
                        word_and(

                            word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                            word_sub(
                              word(27),
                              word(
                                word_clz(

                            word(4294967280)),

                          word_sub(

                            word(

                          word(68)))),

                          word_and(

                                word(

                                    word_and(
                                      hd,
                                      word(4294967292))))))) } else {
                          fl })),
                    fp(gs, ms)))))),
          h)))
```

## TLSF bins as doubly linked lists (tlsf.mbt)

definition `tlsf_dll`

```
tlsf_dll((p : W32), (x : W32), NIL) == ((h : Heap) => x == word(0) && emp(h))
&& tlsf_dll((p : W32), (x : W32), CONS((a : W32), (xs : List[W32]))) == (
     (h : Heap) =>
       x == a
       && !(a == word(0))
       && star(
            pts(val(word_add(a, word(8))), serialise_i32(p)),
            (h1 : Heap) =>
              ∃ n : W32,
                star(
                  pts(val(word_add(a, word(12))), serialise_i32(n)),
                  tlsf_dll(a, n, xs),
                  h1),
            h))
```

definition `tlsf_dll_next`

```
tlsf_dll_next((a : W32), (xs : List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    ∃ n : W32,
      star(
        pts(val(word_add(a, word(12))), serialise_i32(n)),
        tlsf_dll(a, n, xs),
        h))
```

lemma `tlsf_dll_cons`

```
∀ (p x a : W32) (xs : List[W32]),
  tlsf_dll(p, x, CONS(a, xs)) == (
    (h : Heap) =>
      x == a
      && !(a == word(0))
      && star(
           pts(val(word_add(a, word(8))), serialise_i32(p)),
           tlsf_dll_next(a, xs),
           h))
```

definition `tlsf_hd`

```
tlsf_hd(NIL) == word(0)
&& tlsf_hd(CONS((a : W32), (xs : List[W32]))) == a
```

definition `tlsf_bin`

```
tlsf_bin((xs : List[W32])) == tlsf_dll(word(0), tlsf_hd(xs), xs)
```

lemma `star_pure_hr`

```
∀ (c : Bool) (p q : HProp),
  star(p, (h : Heap) => c && q(h)) == ((h : Heap) => c && star(p, q, h))
```

lemma `star_pure_hl`

```
∀ (c : Bool) (p q : HProp),
  star((h : Heap) => c && p(h), q) == ((h : Heap) => c && star(p, q, h))
```

lemma `star_ex_hr`

```
∀ (p : HProp) (qq : (A, Heap) -> Bool),
  star(p, (h : Heap) => ∃ x : A, qq(x, h))
    == ((h : Heap) => ∃ x : A, star(p, qq(x), h))
```

lemma `star_ex_hl`

```
∀ (pp : (A, Heap) -> Bool) (q : HProp),
  star((h : Heap) => ∃ x : A, pp(x, h), q)
    == ((h : Heap) => ∃ x : A, star(pp(x), q, h))
```

lemma `star_emp_hr`

```
∀ (p : HProp), star(p, emp) == p
```

lemma `star_emp_hl`

```
∀ (p : HProp), star(emp, p) == p
```

lemma `sl_pure`

```
∀ (c : Bool) (p q : SAssn) (bs : List[B_e])
if
  1. c → sl_triple(p, bs, q)
then
  sl_triple(
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      c && p(vs, lc, gs, ms, h),
    bs,
    q)
```

lemma `sl_ex`

```
∀ (pp : (A, List[Value], List[Value], List[Value], Num, Heap) -> Bool)
  (bs : List[B_e]) (q : SAssn)
if
  1. ∀ x : A, sl_triple(pp(x), bs, q)
then
  sl_triple(
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ x : A, pp(x, vs, lc, gs, ms, h),
    bs,
    q)
```

lemma `tlsf_push_bin_nil`

```
∀ (d m hd pf0 nf0 fl slb : W32) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
                  serialise_i32(fl)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(4)),
                        word_mul(
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                          word(68)))),
                    serialise_i32(slb)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              word_add(
                                word_and(
                                  word_add(d, word(15)),
                                  word(4294967280)),
                                word(4)),
                              word_mul(
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292))))),
                                word(68))),
                            word(4)),
                          word_jshl(
                            word_and(
                              word_jushr(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                word_sub(
                                  word(27),
                                  word(
                                    word_clz(
                                      word_and(
                                        hd,
                                        word(4294967292)))))),
                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(word(0))),
                    star(tlsf_dll(word(0), word(0), NIL), fp(gs, ms))))))),
          h)),
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [
        V_num(ConstInt32(m)),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_add(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(4)),
              word_mul(
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                word(68))))),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_and(
              word_jushr(
                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))))),
              word(15)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(
              val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
              serialise_i32(
                word_or(
                  fl,
                  word_jshl(
                    word(1),
                    word_sub(
                      word(27),
                      word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))))))),
            star(
              pts(
                val(
                  word_add(
                    word_add(
                      word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                      word(4)),
                    word_mul(
                      word_sub(
                        word(27),
                        word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                      word(68)))),
                serialise_i32(
                  word_or(
                    slb,
                    word_jshl(
                      word(1),
                      word_and(
                        word_jushr(
                          word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(
                              word_clz(
                                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))))),
                        word(15)))))),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(
                    word_add(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_and(
                              word_add(d, word(15)),
                              word(4294967280)),
                            word(4)),
                          word_mul(
                            word_sub(
                              word(27),
                              word(
                                word_clz(
                                  word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                            word(68))),
                        word(4)),
                      word_jshl(
                        word_and(
                          word_jushr(
                            word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),

lemma `tlsf_push_bin_cons`

```

                          word(4)),

                            word(27),
                            word(

                              word(15)),
                            word(2)))),
                      serialise_i32(a)),
                    star(tlsf_dll(word(0), a, CONS(a, ys)), fp(gs, ms))))))),
          h)),
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [
        V_num(ConstInt32(m)),

              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),

              word_mul(
                word_sub(
                  word(27),
                  word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),

                word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),

                  word(27),

                val(

                        word(word_clz(word_and(hd, word(4294967292))))),
                      word(68)))),
                serialise_i32(
                  word_or(
                    slb,
                    word_jshl(
                      word(1),
                      word_and(
                        word_jushr(

                          word_sub(
                            word(27),
                            word(

definition `tlsf_seg`

```
tlsf_seg((p : W32), (x : W32), NIL, (q : W32), (y : W32))
  == ((h : Heap) => x == y && q == p && emp(h))
&& tlsf_seg(
     (p : W32),
     (x : W32),
     CONS((a : W32), (xs : List[W32])),
     (q : W32),
     (y : W32)) == (
     (h : Heap) =>
       x == a
       && !(a == word(0))
       && star(
            pts(val(word_add(a, word(8))), serialise_i32(p)),
            (h1 : Heap) =>
              ∃ n : W32,
                star(
                  pts(val(word_add(a, word(12))), serialise_i32(n)),
                  tlsf_seg(a, n, xs, q, y),
                  h1),
            h))
```

lemma `tlsf_dll_append`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (p x : W32),
  tlsf_dll(p, x, APPEND(ys, zs)) == (
    (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ q : W32,
        ∃ y : W32, star(tlsf_seg(p, x, ys, q, y), tlsf_dll(q, y, zs), h))
```

definition `tlsf_prev`

```
tlsf_prev(NIL) == word(0)
&& tlsf_prev(CONS((a : W32), (t : List[W32])))
     == (if t == NIL { a } else { tlsf_prev(t) })
```

lemma `tlsf_prev_snoc`

```
∀ (l : List[W32]) (a : W32), tlsf_prev(APPEND(l, [a])) == a
```

lemma `tlsf_hd_append`

```
∀ (ys l : List[W32]) (a : W32),
  tlsf_hd(APPEND(ys, CONS(a, l))) == (if ys == NIL { a } else { tlsf_hd(ys) })
```

                  serialise_i32(fl)),
                star(tlsf_bin(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs))), fp(gs, ms))))),
          h)),
    tlsf_pop_code(d),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>

      && star(

```

definition `tlsf_sync`

```
tlsf_sync((s : W32), (n : Num), (ne : (Num) -> Bool))
  == (∀ k : Num, bit(k, s) == (k < n && ne(k)))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_set`

```
∀ (s j : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(j) < n
  2. n <= 32
  3. tlsf_sync(s, n, ne)
then
  tlsf_sync(
    word_or(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)),
    n,
    (k : Num) => k == val(j) || ne(k))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_clear`

```
∀ (s j : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(j) < n
  2. n <= 32
  3. tlsf_sync(s, n, ne)
  4. ne(val(j))
then
  tlsf_sync(
    word_xor(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)),
    n,
    (k : Num) => !(k == val(j)) && ne(k))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_zero`

```
∀ (s : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_sync(s, n, ne)
then
  (s == word(0)) == (∀ k : Num, k < n → !ne(k))
```

lemma `tlsf_code_bounds`

```
∀ (x : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(x)
then
  val(word_and(word_jushr(x, word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(x)))), word(15))) < 16
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
         (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
         (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(x)))) < 28
```

## TLSF all bins and bitmaps (tlsf_bins.mbt)

definition `tlsf_istar`

```
tlsf_istar((ff : (Num, Heap) -> Bool), 0) == emp
&& tlsf_istar((ff : (Num, Heap) -> Bool), SUC((n : Num)))
     == star(tlsf_istar(ff, n), ff(n))
```

lemma `tlsf_istar_cong`

```
∀ (n : Num) (ff gg : (Num, Heap) -> Bool)
if
  1. ∀ i : Num, i < n → ff(i) == gg(i)
then
  tlsf_istar(ff, n) == tlsf_istar(gg, n)
```

lemma `tlsf_istar_focus`

```
∀ (n k : Num) (ff : (Num, Heap) -> Bool)
if
  1. k < n
then
  tlsf_istar(ff, n)
    == star(ff(k), tlsf_istar((i : Num) => if i == k { emp } else { ff(i) }, n))
```

definition `tlsf_upd`

```
tlsf_upd((ff : (Num) -> A), (k : Num), (v : A))
  == ((i : Num) => if i == k { v } else { ff(i) })
```

definition `tlsf_slot_item`

```
tlsf_slot_item((d : W32), (fw : W32), (j : Num), (xs : List[W32])) == star(
  pts(
    val(
      word_add(
        word_add(
          word_add(
            word_add(
              word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
              word(4)),
            word_mul(fw, word(68))),
          word(4)),
        word_jshl(word(j), word(2)))),
    serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(xs))),
  tlsf_bin(xs))
```

definition `tlsf_level`

```
tlsf_level((d : W32), (f : Num), (s : W32), (L : (Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_sync(s, 16, (j : Num) => !(L(j) == NIL))
    && star(
         pts(
           val(
             word_add(
               word_add(
                 word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                 word(4)),
               word_mul(word(f), word(68)))),
           serialise_i32(s)),
         tlsf_istar((j : Num) => tlsf_slot_item(d, word(f), j, L(j)), 16),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_bins`

```
tlsf_bins(
  (d : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_sync(fl, 28, (f : Num) => !(sb(f) == word(0)))
    && star(
         pts(
           val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))),
           serialise_i32(fl)),
         tlsf_istar((f : Num) => tlsf_level(d, f, sb(f), bl(f)), 28),
         h))
```

lemma `tlsf_upd_same`

```
∀ (ff : (Num) -> A) (k : Num) (v : A), tlsf_upd(ff, k, v, k) == v
```

lemma `tlsf_level_rest`

```
∀ (d v : W32) (k : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (L : (Num) -> List[W32]),
  (
    (i : Num) =>
      if i == k { emp } else {
        tlsf_level(d, i, tlsf_upd(sb, k, v, i), tlsf_upd(bl, k, L, i)) })
    == ((i : Num) => if i == k { emp } else { tlsf_level(d, i, sb(i), bl(i)) })
```

lemma `tlsf_item_rest`

```
∀ (d fw : W32) (k : Num) (L : (Num) -> List[W32]) (xs : List[W32]),
  (
    (j : Num) =>
      if j
        == k { emp } else { tlsf_slot_item(d, fw, j, tlsf_upd(L, k, xs, j)) })
    == ((j : Num) => if j == k { emp } else { tlsf_slot_item(d, fw, j, L(j)) })
```

lemma `tlsf_or_bit_nz`

```
∀ (x j : W32), !(word_or(x, word_jshl(word(1), j)) == word(0))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_push_bin`

```
∀ (s j m : W32) (L : (Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. val(j) < 16
  2. tlsf_sync(s, 16, (i : Num) => !(L(i) == NIL))
then
  tlsf_sync(
    word_or(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)),
    16,
    (i : Num) => !(tlsf_upd(L, val(j), CONS(m, L(val(j))), i) == NIL))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_push_top`

```
∀ (fl f j : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
if
  1. val(f) < 28
  2. tlsf_sync(fl, 28, (i : Num) => !(sb(i) == word(0)))
then
  tlsf_sync(
    word_or(fl, word_jshl(word(1), f)),
    28,
    (i : Num) =>
      !(
        tlsf_upd(sb, val(f), word_or(sb(val(f)), word_jshl(word(1), j)), i)
          == word(0)))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_replace`

```
∀ (s j : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool) (b : Bool)
if
  1. val(j) < n
  2. n <= 32
  3. tlsf_sync(s, n, ne)
  4. ne(val(j))
then
  tlsf_sync(
    if b { s } else { word_xor(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)) },
    n,
    (k : Num) => if k == val(j) { b } else { ne(k) })
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_pop_bin`

```
∀ (s j : W32) (L : (Num) -> List[W32]) (xs : List[W32])
if
  1. val(j) < 16
  2. tlsf_sync(s, 16, (i : Num) => !(L(i) == NIL))
  3. !(L(val(j)) == NIL)
then
  tlsf_sync(
    if xs == NIL { word_xor(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)) } else { s },
    16,
    (i : Num) => !(tlsf_upd(L, val(j), xs, i) == NIL))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_pop_top`

```
∀ (fl f s : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
if
  1. val(f) < 28
  2. tlsf_sync(fl, 28, (i : Num) => !(sb(i) == word(0)))
  3. !(sb(val(f)) == word(0))
then
  tlsf_sync(
    if s == word(0) { word_xor(fl, word_jshl(word(1), f)) } else { fl },
    28,
    (i : Num) => !(tlsf_upd(sb, val(f), s, i) == word(0)))
```

lemma `tlsf_sync_occupied_nz`

```
∀ (s : W32) (n k : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_sync(s, n, ne)
  2. k < n
  3. ne(k)
then
  !(s == word(0))
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_bitmap`

```
∀ (fl s f j : W32) (empty : Bool)
if
  1. !(s == word(0))
then
  (
    if empty {
      if word_xor(s, word_jshl(word(1), j))
        == word(0) { word_xor(fl, word_jshl(word(1), f)) } else { fl } } else {
      fl }) == (
    if (if empty { word_xor(s, word_jshl(word(1), j)) } else { s })
      == word(0) { word_xor(fl, word_jshl(word(1), f)) } else { fl })
```

example `tlsf_push_all`

```
∀ (d m hd pf0 nf0 fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
  2. 16 <= val(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(
              (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  m,
                  (word : (Num) -> W32)(8))),
              serialise_i32(pf0)),
            star(
              pts(
                (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                  (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                    m,
                    (word : (Num) -> W32)(12))),
                serialise_i32(nf0)),
              star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
          h)),
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      vs == [V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0)))]
      && star(
           pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
           star(
             tlsf_bins(
               d,
               (word_or : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                 fl,
                 (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(

                 (word_or : (W32, W32) -> W32)(

                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                           (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               hd,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292))))))),
                   (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(1),
                     (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           hd,
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)),
                         (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                             (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                               (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                 hd,
                                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)))))),
                       (word : (Num) -> W32)(15))))),
               (
                 tlsf_upd : (
                   (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                   Num,
                   (Num) -> List[W32],
                   Num,
                   Num) -> List[W32])(
                 bl,

example `tlsf_pop_all`

```
∀ (d m hd fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (ys zs : List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)))
  2. bl(
       (val : (W32) -> Num)(
         (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
           (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
           (word : (Num) -> W32)(
             (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
               (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                 hd,
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)))))),
       (val : (W32) -> Num)(
         (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
           (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               hd,
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)),
             (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                 (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                     hd,
                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)))))),
           (word : (Num) -> W32)(15)))) == (
       APPEND : (List[W32], List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
       ys,
       (CONS : (W32, List[W32]) -> List[W32])(m, zs))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        star(
          pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)),
          h)),
    tlsf_pop_code(d),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      vs == (NIL : List[Value])
      && star(
           pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
           star(
             pts(
               (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                 (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                   m,
                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(8))),
               serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(ys))),
             star(
               pts(
                 (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                   (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                     m,
                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(12))),
                 serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(zs))),
               star(
                 tlsf_bins(
                   d,
                   if (APPEND : (List[W32], List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
                     ys,
                     zs) == (NIL : List[W32]) {
                     if (word_xor : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       sb(
                         (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                           (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                               (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                 (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                   hd,
                                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                     4294967292))))))),
                       (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(1),
                         (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               hd,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)),
                             (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                 (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                     hd,
                                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                       4294967292)))))),
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(15)))) == (
                       word : (Num) -> W32)(0) {
                       (word_xor : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         fl,
                         (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(1),
                           (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                               (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                 (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                   hd,
                                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                     4294967292))))))) } else { fl } } else {
                     fl },
                   (tlsf_upd : ((Num) -> W32, Num, W32, Num) -> W32)(
                     sb,
                     (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                       (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                           (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               hd,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)))))),
                     if (APPEND : (List[W32], List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
                       ys,
                       zs) == (NIL : List[W32]) {
                       (word_xor : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         sb(
                           (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                             (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                 (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                     hd,
                                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                       4294967292))))))),
                         (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(1),
                           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                 hd,
                                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)),
                               (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                   (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                     (
                                       word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                       hd,
                                       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                         4294967292)))))),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(15)))) } else {
                       sb(
                         (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                           (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                               (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                 (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                   hd,
                                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                     4294967292))))))) }),
                   (
                     tlsf_upd : (
                       (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                       Num,
                       (Num) -> List[W32],
                       Num,
                       Num) -> List[W32])(
                     bl,
                     (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                       (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                           (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               hd,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)))))),
                     (
                       tlsf_upd : (
                         (Num) -> List[W32],
                         Num,
                         List[W32],
                         Num) -> List[W32])(
                       bl(
                         (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                           (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                               (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                 (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                   hd,
                                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                     4294967292))))))),
                       (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                         (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                           (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               hd,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967292)),
                             (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                 (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(
                                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                     hd,
                                     (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                                       4294967292)))))),
                           (word : (Num) -> W32)(15))),
                       (APPEND : (List[W32], List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
                         ys,
                         zs)))),
                 fp(gs, ms)))),
           h))
```

## TLSF physical chain and bin correspondence (tlsf_physical.mbt)

definition `tlsf_size`

```
tlsf_size((hd : W32)) == word_and(hd, word(4294967292))
```

definition `tlsf_used`

```
tlsf_used((hd : W32)) == !(word_and(hd, word(1)) == word(0))
```

definition `tlsf_last`

```
tlsf_last((hd : W32)) == !(word_and(hd, word(2)) == word(0))
```

definition `tlsf_level_of`

```
tlsf_level_of((hd : W32)) == (val : (W32) -> Num)(
  (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
    (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd)))))
```

definition `tlsf_slot_of`

```
tlsf_slot_of((hd : W32)) == val(
  word_and(
    word_jushr(
      tlsf_size(hd),
      word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(tlsf_size(hd))))),
    word(15)))
```

lemma `tlsf_header_class_bounds`

```
∀ (hd : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
then
  tlsf_level_of(hd) < 28
  && tlsf_slot_of(hd) < 16
```

lemma `tlsf_header_class`

```
∀ (hd : W32),
  tlsf_level_of(hd) == val(word_sub(tlsf_fli(tlsf_size(hd)), word(4)))
  && tlsf_slot_of(hd) == val(tlsf_sli(tlsf_size(hd), tlsf_fli(tlsf_size(hd))))
```

definition `tlsf_block_ok`

```
tlsf_block_ok((a : Num), (lim : Num), (m : W32), (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
  val(m) == a
  && !(m == word(0))
  && MOD(a, 8) == 0
  && 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  && MOD(val(tlsf_size(hd(m))), 8) == 0
  && a + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) <= lim)
```

definition `tlsf_layout`

```
tlsf_layout(
  (p : W32),
  (a : Num),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  NIL,
  (hd : (W32) -> W32))
  == (a == lim && p == top)
&& tlsf_layout(
     (p : W32),
     (a : Num),
     (lim : Num),
     (top : W32),
     CONS((m : W32), (xs : List[W32])),
     (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
     tlsf_block_ok(a, lim, m, hd)
     && tlsf_last(hd(m)) == (xs == NIL)
     && tlsf_layout(m, a + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))), lim, top, xs, hd))
```

lemma `tlsf_block_field_address`

```
∀ (a lim k : Num) (m : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_block_ok(a, lim, m, hd)
  2. lim <= 4294967296
  3. k <= 16
then
  val(word_add(m, word(k))) == val(m) + k
```

lemma `tlsf_block_spare_end`

```
∀ (a lim : Num) (m : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_block_ok(a, lim, m, hd)
then
  val(m) + 16 + (val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) - 8)
    == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_lower`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top m : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
then
  a <= val(m)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_members`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top m : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
then
  tlsf_block_ok(val(m), lim, m, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_order`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
then
  PAIRWISE(
    (m : W32) => (n : W32) => val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) <= val(n),
    xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_distinct`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
then
  PAIRWISE((m : W32) => (n : W32) => !(m == n), xs)
```

definition `tlsf_count`

```
tlsf_count((m : W32), (xs : List[W32]))
  == LENGTH(FILTER((x : W32) => x == m, xs))
```

lemma `tlsf_count_member`

```
∀ (m : W32) (xs : List[W32]), MEM(m, xs) == (0 < tlsf_count(m, xs))
```

lemma `tlsf_count_zero`

```
∀ (m : W32) (xs : List[W32]), (tlsf_count(m, xs) == 0) == !MEM(m, xs)
```

definition `tlsf_bins_match`

```
tlsf_bins_match(
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (
    ∀ m : W32,
      MEM(m, xs)
      && !tlsf_used(hd(m)) → tlsf_count(
           m,
           bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))))
           == 1)
  && (
       ∀ f : Num,
         ∀ j : Num,
           ∀ m : W32,
             f < 28
             && j < 16
             && MEM(m, bl(f, j)) → MEM(m, xs)
             && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
             && tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == f
             && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == j))
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_exact`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (m : W32)
  (f j : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. f < 28
  3. j < 16
then
  tlsf_count(m, bl(f, j)) == (
    if MEM(m, xs)
    && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == f
    && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == j { 1 } else { 0 })
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_member`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (m : W32)
  (f j : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. f < 28
  3. j < 16
then
  MEM(m, bl(f, j)) == (
    MEM(m, xs)
    && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == f
    && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == j)
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_unique`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (m : W32)
  (f j g k : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. f < 28
  3. j < 16
  4. g < 28
  5. k < 16
  6. MEM(m, bl(f, j))
  7. MEM(m, bl(g, k))
then
  f == g
  && j == k
  && tlsf_count(m, bl(f, j)) == 1
```

definition `tlsf_bytes`

```
tlsf_bytes((a : Num), (n : Num))
  == ((h : Heap) => ∃ bs : List[Byte], LENGTH(bs) == n && pts(a, bs, h))
```

definition `tlsf_spare`

```
tlsf_spare((m : W32), (hd : W32)) == (
  if tlsf_used(hd) { emp } else {
    tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 16, val(tlsf_size(hd)) - 8) })
```

definition `tlsf_regions`

```
tlsf_regions((p : W32), NIL, (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == emp
&& tlsf_regions(
     (p : W32),
     CONS((m : W32), (xs : List[W32])),
     (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == star(
     pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
     star(
       pts(val(word_add(m, word(4))), serialise_i32(p)),
       star(tlsf_spare(m, hd(m)), tlsf_regions(m, xs, hd))))
```

definition `tlsf_base`

```
tlsf_base((d : W32)) == word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))
```

definition `tlsf_origin`

```
tlsf_origin((d : W32)) == val(tlsf_base(d)) + 2176
```

definition `tlsf_coalesced`

```
tlsf_coalesced(NIL, (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == true
&& tlsf_coalesced(CONS((m : W32), (xs : List[W32])), (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
     (xs == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(m)) || tlsf_used(hd(HD(xs))))
     && tlsf_coalesced(xs, hd))
```

definition `tlsf_physical_ok`

```
tlsf_physical_ok(
  (d : W32),
  (ms : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
  val(d) <= 4294965088
  && ms <= 4294967296
  && MOD(ms, 65536) == 0
  && !(xs == NIL)
  && tlsf_layout(word(0), tlsf_origin(d), ms, top, xs, hd)
  && tlsf_coalesced(xs, hd))
```

definition `tlsf_chain`

```
tlsf_chain(
  (d : W32),
  (ms : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_physical_ok(d, ms, top, xs, hd)
    && star(
         pts(val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))), serialise_i32(top)),
         tlsf_regions(word(0), xs, hd),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_large`

```
tlsf_large(
  (d : W32),
  (ms : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
    && star(tlsf_chain(d, ms, top, xs, hd), tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), h))
```

lemma `tlsf_large_layout`

```
∀ (d top fl : W32) (ms : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_large(d, ms, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
then
  tlsf_layout(word(0), tlsf_origin(d), ms, top, xs, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_large_free_exact`

```
∀ (d top fl m : W32) (ms f j : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_large(d, ms, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
  2. f < 28
  3. j < 16
then
  tlsf_count(m, bl(f, j)) == (
    if MEM(m, xs)
    && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == f
    && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == j { 1 } else { 0 })
```

lemma `tlsf_large_order`

```
∀ (d top fl : W32) (ms : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_large(d, ms, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
then
  PAIRWISE(
    (m : W32) => (n : W32) => val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) <= val(n),
    xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_large_free_once`

```
∀ (d top fl m : W32) (ms : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_large(d, ms, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
then
  tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) < 28
  && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) < 16
  && tlsf_count(m, bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)))) == 1
```

## Compiled TLSF search (tlsf_find_code.mbt)

program `tlsf_find_code`

```
tlsf_find_code((d : W32)) ==
  block (result i32)
    i32.const 31
    i32.const 1
    i32.const 27
    local.get 0
    i32.const 15
    i32.add
    i32.const 4294967280
    i32.and
    local.tee 3
    local.tee 0
    i32.clz
    i32.sub
    i32.shl
    i32.const 1
    i32.sub
    local.tee 1
    local.get 0
    i32.add
    local.get 1
    i32.const 4294967295
    i32.xor
    i32.and
    local.tee 0
    i32.clz
    i32.sub
    local.tee 1
    i32.const 4
    i32.sub
    local.set 2
    i32.const d
    i32.const 15
    i32.add
    i32.const 4294967280
    i32.and
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    local.tee 4
    i32.const 1
    local.get 1
    i32.const 4
    i32.sub
    i32.shl
    i32.const 1
    i32.sub
    i32.const 4294967295
    i32.xor
    local.tee 1
    i32.and
    i32.ctz
    local.tee 5
    i32.const 32
    i32.ne
    if
      i32.const d
      i32.const 15
      i32.add
      i32.const 4294967280
      i32.and
      i32.const 4
      i32.add
      local.get 5
      i32.const 68
      i32.mul
      i32.add
      local.tee 5
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 1
      local.get 0
      local.get 2
      i32.shr_u
      i32.const 15
      i32.and
      i32.shl
      i32.const 1
      i32.sub
      i32.const 4294967295
      i32.xor
      i32.and
      i32.ctz
      local.tee 0
      i32.const 32
      i32.ne
      if
        local.get 5
        i32.const 4
        i32.add
        local.get 0
        i32.const 2
        i32.shl
        i32.add
        i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
        br 2
      else
        local.get 4
        local.get 1
        i32.const 1
        i32.shl
        i32.and
        i32.ctz
        local.tee 0
        i32.const 32
        i32.ne
        if
          i32.const d
          i32.const 15
          i32.add
          i32.const 4294967280
          i32.and
          i32.const 4
          i32.add
          local.get 0
          i32.const 68
          i32.mul
          i32.add
          local.tee 0
          i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
          i32.ctz
          local.tee 1
          i32.const 32
          i32.ne
          if
            local.get 0
            i32.const 4
            i32.add
            local.get 1
            i32.const 2
            i32.shl
            i32.add
            i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
            br 4
          end
        end
      end
    end
    i32.const 0
  end
```

## TLSF bitmap search (tlsf_find_bits.mbt, tlsf_find_reads.mbt, tlsf_find.mbt)

lemma `tlsf_xor_ones`

```
∀ (x : W32), word_xor(x, word(4294967295)) == word_not(x)
```

definition `tlsf_cut`

```
tlsf_cut((k : W32))
  == word_xor(word_sub(word_jshl(word(1), k), word(1)), word(4294967295))
```

lemma `tlsf_cut_not`

```
∀ (k : W32), tlsf_cut(k) == word_not(word_sub(word_jshl(word(1), k), word(1)))
```

lemma `tlsf_cut_bits`

```
∀ (k : W32) (i : Num)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
then
  bit(i, tlsf_cut(k)) == (i < 32 && val(k) <= i)
```

lemma `tlsf_cut_next_bits`

```
∀ (k : W32) (i : Num)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
then
  bit(i, word_jshl(tlsf_cut(k), word(1))) == (i < 32 && val(k) < i)
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_value`

```
∀ (x : W32),
  (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)((word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x)))
    == (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x)
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_test`

```
∀ (x : W32),
  (
    (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x))
      == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
    == ((word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x) == 32)
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_hit`

```
∀ (x : W32)
if
  1. word_ctz(x) < 32
then
  bit(word_ctz(x), x)
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_select`

```
∀ (x mask : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  2. !(word_ctz(word_and(x, mask)) == 32)
then
  word_ctz(word_and(x, mask)) < n
  && ne(word_ctz(word_and(x, mask)))
  && bit(word_ctz(word_and(x, mask)), mask)
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_range`

```
∀ (x mask : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  2. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)((word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(x, mask)))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)((word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(x, mask)))) < n
```

lemma `tlsf_ctz_plain_range`

```
∀ (x : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  2. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)((word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(x))) < n
```

lemma `tlsf_masked_select`

```
∀ (x k : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
  2. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  3. !(word_ctz(word_and(x, tlsf_cut(k))) == 32)
then
  word_ctz(word_and(x, tlsf_cut(k))) < n
  && ne(word_ctz(word_and(x, tlsf_cut(k))))
  && val(k) <= word_ctz(word_and(x, tlsf_cut(k)))
```

lemma `tlsf_next_select`

```
∀ (x k : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
  2. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  3. !(word_ctz(word_and(x, word_jshl(tlsf_cut(k), word(1)))) == 32)
then
  word_ctz(word_and(x, word_jshl(tlsf_cut(k), word(1)))) < n
  && ne(word_ctz(word_and(x, word_jshl(tlsf_cut(k), word(1)))))
  && val(k) < word_ctz(word_and(x, word_jshl(tlsf_cut(k), word(1))))
```

lemma `tlsf_masked_index`

```
∀ (x k : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
  2. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  3. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(x, tlsf_cut(k))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(x, tlsf_cut(k))))) < n
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(k) <= (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(x, tlsf_cut(k)))))
```

lemma `tlsf_next_index`

```
∀ (x k : W32) (n : Num) (ne : (Num) -> Bool)
if
  1. val(k) < 32
  2. tlsf_sync(x, n, ne)
  3. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             x,
             (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               tlsf_cut(k),
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          x,
          (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            tlsf_cut(k),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))) < n
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(k) < (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             x,
             (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               tlsf_cut(k),
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(1))))))
```

definition `tlsf_tables_sync`

```
tlsf_tables_sync(
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  tlsf_sync(fl, 28, (f : Num) => !(sb(f) == word(0)))
  && (
       ∀ f : Num,
         f < 28 → tlsf_sync(sb(f), 16, (j : Num) => !(bl(f, j) == NIL))))
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_sync`

```
∀ (d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl, h)
then
  tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
```

definition `tlsf_ro`

```
tlsf_ro(
  (d : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (before : Heap),
  (fp : GProp),
  (gs : List[Value]),
  (ms : Num),
  (h : Heap))
  == (h == before && star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms), h))
```

lemma `tlsf_ro_fl`

```
∀ (d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp) (gs : List[Value]) (ms : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp, gs, ms, h)
then
  has_i32(h, val(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))), fl)
```

lemma `tlsf_ro_sb`

```
∀ (d fl fw : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp) (gs : List[Value]) (ms : Num)
if
  1. val(fw) < 28
  2. tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp, gs, ms, h)
then
  has_i32(
    h,
    val(
      word_add(
        word_add(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)), word(4)),
        word_mul(fw, word(68)))),
    sb(val(fw)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ro_head`

```
∀ (d fl fw jw : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp) (gs : List[Value]) (ms : Num)
if
  1. val(fw) < 28
  2. val(jw) < 16
  3. tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp, gs, ms, h)
then
  has_i32(
    h,
    val(
      word_add(
        word_add(
          word_add(
            word_add(
              word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
              word(4)),
            word_mul(fw, word(68))),
          word(4)),
        word_jshl(jw, word(2)))),
    tlsf_hd(bl(val(fw), val(jw))))
```

lemma `st_tlsf_load_fl`

```
∀ (d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before : Heap) (fp : GProp) (v lc : List[Value]),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(
        v,
        [
          V_num(
            ConstInt32(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)),
    [Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(fl))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)))
```

lemma `st_tlsf_load_sb`

```
∀ (d fl fw : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before : Heap) (fp : GProp) (v lc : List[Value])
if
  1. val(fw) < 28
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(
        v,
        [
          V_num(
            ConstInt32(
              word_add(
                word_add(
                  word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                  word(4)),
                word_mul(fw, word(68)))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)),
    [Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(sb(val(fw))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)))
```

lemma `st_tlsf_load_head`

```
∀ (d fl fw jw : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before : Heap) (fp : GProp) (v lc : List[Value])
if
  1. val(fw) < 28
  2. val(jw) < 16
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(
        v,
        [
          V_num(
            ConstInt32(
              word_add(
                word_add(
                  word_add(
                    word_add(
                      word_and(
                        word_add(d, word(15)),
                        word(4294967280)),
                      word(4)),
                    word_mul(fw, word(68))),
                  word(4)),
                word_jshl(jw, word(2)))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)),
    [Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_hd(bl(val(fw), val(jw)))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)))
```

definition `tlsf_align`

```
tlsf_align((s : W32)) == word_and(word_add(s, word(15)), word(4294967280))
```

lemma `tlsf_fword_code`

```
∀ (x : W32),
  (word_sub : (Word[_], Word[_]) -> Word[_])(
    (word_sub : (Word[_], Word[_]) -> Word[_])(
      (word : (Num) -> Word[_])(31),
      (word : (Num) -> Word[_])(word_clz(x))),
    (word : (Num) -> Word[_])(4)) == (word_sub : (Word[_], Word[_]) -> Word[_])(
    (word : (Num) -> Word[_])(27),
    (word : (Num) -> Word[_])(word_clz(x)))
```

lemma `tlsf_round_code`

```
∀ (s : W32),
  word_and(
    word_add(
      word_sub(
        word_jshl(word(1), word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(s)))),
        word(1)),
      s),
    word_xor(
      word_sub(
        word_jshl(word(1), word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(s)))),
        word(1)),
      word(4294967295)))
    == tlsf_round(s)
```

definition `tlsf_search_f`

```
tlsf_search_f((s : W32))
  == word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(tlsf_round(tlsf_align(s)))))
```

definition `tlsf_search_j`

```
tlsf_search_j((s : W32))
  == word_and(word_jushr(tlsf_round(tlsf_align(s)), tlsf_search_f(s)), word(15))
```

definition `tlsf_search_domain`

```
tlsf_search_domain((s : W32), (fl : W32), (sb : (Num) -> W32)) == (
  (
    !(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(
        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))
        == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32)) → (val : (W32) -> Num)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(
        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))) < 28)
  && (
       !(
         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
           (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))
           == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
       && !(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  sb(
                    (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                      (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                            fl,
                            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
                  tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s)))))
              == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32)) → (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  sb(
                    (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                      (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                            fl,
                            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
                  tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s)))))) < 16)
  && (
       !(
         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
           (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               fl,
               (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                 tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))
           == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32)) → (val : (W32) -> Num)(
         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
           (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               fl,
               (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                 tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))) < 28)
  && (
       !(
         (word : (Num) -> W32)(
           (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
             (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               fl,
               (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                 tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))
           == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
       && !(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                sb(
                  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                          fl,
                          (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
