                            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                            (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))))))
              == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32)) → (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                sb(
                  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                          fl,
                          (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                            (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))))))) < 16))
```

definition `tlsf_search`

```
tlsf_search(
  (s : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  if (word : (Num) -> W32)(
    (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
      (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))) == (
    word : (Num) -> W32)(32) { (word : (Num) -> W32)(0) } else {
    if (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          sb(
            (val : (W32) -> Num)(
              (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                  (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                    fl,
                    tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
          tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s))))) == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32) {
      if (word : (Num) -> W32)(
        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            fl,
            (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
              tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
              (word : (Num) -> W32)(1))))) == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32) {
        (word : (Num) -> W32)(0) } else {
        if (word : (Num) -> W32)(
          (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
            sb(
              (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                  (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                    (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                      fl,
                      (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                        tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                        (word : (Num) -> W32)(1))))))))) == (
          word : (Num) -> W32)(32) { (word : (Num) -> W32)(0) } else {
          tlsf_hd(
            bl(
              (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                  (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                    (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                      fl,
                      (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                        tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                        (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))),
              (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                  (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                    sb(
                      (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                        (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                          (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                            (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                              fl,
                              (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                                tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                                (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))))))))) } } } else {
      tlsf_hd(
        bl(
          (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  fl,
                  tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))),
          (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  sb(
                    (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                      (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                        (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                            fl,
                            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
                  tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s)))))))) } })
```

lemma `tlsf_search_domain_sync`

```
∀ (s fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
then
  tlsf_search_domain(s, fl, sb)
```

example `tlsf_find_query`

```
∀ (s d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. tlsf_search_domain(s, fl, sb)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp)),
    tlsf_find_code(d),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_align(s)))
      && vs == [V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl)))]
      && tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp, gs, ms, h))
```

## Physical search result (tlsf_find_spec.mbt)

lemma `tlsf_align_value`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(tlsf_align(s)) == 16 * DIV(val(s) + 15, 16)
```

lemma `tlsf_align_bounds`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(s) <= val(tlsf_align(s))
  && val(tlsf_align(s)) <= 4160749568
```

lemma `tlsf_code_index_value`

```
∀ (x : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(x)
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
      (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(x))))
    == (val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_fli(x)) - 4
```

lemma `tlsf_search_size_sound`

```
∀ (s hd : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  4. val(tlsf_search_f(s)) < tlsf_level_of(hd)
     || tlsf_level_of(hd) == val(tlsf_search_f(s))
        && val(tlsf_search_j(s)) <= tlsf_slot_of(hd)
then
  val(s) <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
```

lemma `tlsf_head_member`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32])
if
  1. !(tlsf_hd(xs) == word(0))
then
  MEM(tlsf_hd(xs), xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_bin_fit`

```
∀ (s p top m : W32) (a lim f j : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  3. 16 <= val(s)
  4. val(s) <= 4160749568
  5. f < 28
  6. j < 16
  7. m == tlsf_hd(bl(f, j))
  8. !(m == word(0))
  9. val(tlsf_search_f(s)) < f
     || f == val(tlsf_search_f(s)) && val(tlsf_search_j(s)) <= j
then
  MEM(m, xs)
  && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  && val(s) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
```

lemma `tlsf_search_class_bounds`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(tlsf_search_f(s)) < 28
  && val(tlsf_search_j(s)) < 16
```

lemma `tlsf_search_direct_class`

```
∀ (s fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
  2. 16 <= val(s)
  3. val(s) <= 4160749568
  4. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
  5. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             sb(
               (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                   (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                     (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       fl,
                       tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
             tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s)))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)))))) < 28
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             sb(
               (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                   (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                     (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       fl,
                       tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
             tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s)))))) < 16
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_search_f(s)) < (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))
     || (val : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word : (Num) -> W32)(
            (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
              (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(fl, tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))
          == (val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_search_f(s))
        && (val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_search_j(s)) <= (val : (W32) -> Num)(
             (word : (Num) -> W32)(
               (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                 (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                   sb(
                     (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             fl,
                             tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s))))))),
                   tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_j(s))))))
```

lemma `tlsf_search_next_class`

```
∀ (s fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
  2. 16 <= val(s)
  3. val(s) <= 4160749568
  4. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             fl,
             (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
  5. !(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           sb(
             (val : (W32) -> Num)(
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                 (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                     fl,
                     (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                       (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))))))
         == (word : (Num) -> W32)(32))
then
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(
      (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          fl,
          (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))) < 28
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           sb(
             (val : (W32) -> Num)(
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                 (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
                   (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                     fl,
                     (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                       tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
                       (word : (Num) -> W32)(1)))))))))) < 16
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_search_f(s)) < (val : (W32) -> Num)(
       (word : (Num) -> W32)(
         (word_ctz : (W32) -> Num)(
           (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
             fl,
             (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
               tlsf_cut(tlsf_search_f(s)),
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(1))))))
```

lemma `tlsf_search_sound`

```
∀ (s fl p top : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (a lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
  2. 16 <= val(s)
  3. val(s) <= 4160749568
  4. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
  5. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
then
  tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl) == word(0)
or
  MEM(tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl), xs)
  && !tlsf_used(hd(tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl)))
  && val(s) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl))))
```

definition `tlsf_find_result`

```
tlsf_find_result((s : W32), (xs : List[W32]), (hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32)) == (
  m == word(0)
  || MEM(m, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(m)) && val(s) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m))))
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_sync_frame`

```
∀ (d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (p : HProp)
  (h : Heap)
if
  1. star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), p, h)
then
  tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_ro_domain`

```
∀ (s d fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp) (gs : List[Value]) (ms : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_ro(d, fl, sb, bl, before, fp, gs, ms, h)
then
  tlsf_search_domain(s, fl, sb)
```

lemma `tlsf_large_tables`

```
∀ (d top fl : W32) (limit : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_large(d, limit, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
then
  tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_large_search_sound`

```
∀ (s d top fl : W32) (limit : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (h : Heap)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. tlsf_large(d, limit, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, h)
then
  tlsf_find_result(s, xs, hd, tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl))
```

lemma `tlsf_large_search_sound_frame`

```
∀ (s d top fl : W32) (limit : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (p : HProp) (h : Heap)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. star(tlsf_large(d, limit, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), p, h)
then
  tlsf_find_result(s, xs, hd, tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl))
```

## Search against the complete invariant (tlsf_find_all.mbt)

example `tlsf_find_all`

```
∀ (s d top fl : W32) (limit : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap)
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == limit
        && h == before
        && star(tlsf_large(d, limit, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms), h)),
    tlsf_find_code(d),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_align(s)))
      && (
           (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
             ms == limit
             && h == before
             && star(
                  tlsf_large(d, limit, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
                  fp(gs, ms),
                  h))(gs, ms, h)
      && (
           ∃ m : W32,
             vs == [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]
             && m == tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl)
             && tlsf_find_result(s, xs, hd, m)))
```

## Compiled TLSF split (tlsf_split_code.mbt)

program `tlsf_split_code`

```
tlsf_split_code((d : W32), (push : Num)) ==
  local.get 0
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 4294967292
  i32.and
  local.tee 2
  local.get 3
  i32.const 264
  i32.add
  i32.gt_u
  if
    local.get 0
    local.get 2
    i32.add
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    local.set 4
    local.get 3
    local.get 0
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    i32.add
    local.tee 1
    local.get 2
    local.get 3
    i32.sub
    i32.const 8
    i32.sub
    local.get 1
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 3
    i32.and
    i32.or
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 1
    local.get 1
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 4294967294
    i32.and
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 1
    i32.const 4
    i32.add
    local.get 0
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 1
    call push
    drop
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 2
    i32.and
    i32.const 0
    i32.ne
    local.tee 2
    if
      local.get 1
      local.get 1
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 4294967293
      i32.and
      i32.const 2
      i32.or
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    else
      local.get 1
      local.get 1
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 4294967293
      i32.and
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    end
    local.get 2
    if
      local.get 0
      local.get 0
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 4294967293
      i32.and
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const d
      i32.const 15
      i32.add
      i32.const 4294967280
      i32.and
      i32.const 1908
      i32.add
      local.get 1
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    else
      local.get 4
      i32.const 4
      i32.add
      local.get 1
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    end
    local.get 0
    local.get 3
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 3
    i32.and
    i32.or
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
  end
  local.get 0
  local.get 0
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 4294967294
  i32.and
  i32.const 1
  i32.or
  i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
```

## TLSF split: structural lemmas and verified calls (tlsf_split_*.mbt)

definition `tlsf_header`

```
tlsf_header((s : W32), (last : Bool), (used : Bool)) == word_or(
  s,
  word_or(
    if last { word(2) } else { word(0) },
    if used { word(1) } else { word(0) }))
```

lemma `tlsf_and_or`

```
∀ (x y z : W32),
  word_and(word_or(x, y), z) == word_or(word_and(x, z), word_and(y, z))
```

lemma `tlsf_and_assoc`

```
∀ (x y z : W32), word_and(word_and(x, y), z) == word_and(x, word_and(y, z))
```

lemma `tlsf_header_masks`

```
∀ (h : W32) (last used : Bool),
  tlsf_size(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used)) == tlsf_size(h)
  && word_and(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used), word(2))
       == (if last { word(2) } else { word(0) })
  && word_and(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used), word(1))
       == (if used { word(1) } else { word(0) })
```

lemma `tlsf_header_fields`

```
∀ (h : W32) (last used : Bool),
  tlsf_size(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used)) == tlsf_size(h)
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used)) == last
  && tlsf_used(tlsf_header(tlsf_size(h), last, used)) == used
```

definition `tlsf_end`

```
tlsf_end((p : W32), NIL) == p
&& tlsf_end((p : W32), CONS((m : W32), (xs : List[W32]))) == tlsf_end(m, xs)
```

definition `tlsf_path`

```
tlsf_path(NIL, (a : Num), (lim : Num), (hd : (W32) -> W32), (b : Num))
  == (a == b)
&& tlsf_path(
     CONS((m : W32), (xs : List[W32])),
     (a : Num),
     (lim : Num),
     (hd : (W32) -> W32),
     (b : Num)) == (
     tlsf_block_ok(a, lim, m, hd)
     && !tlsf_last(hd(m))
     && tlsf_path(xs, a + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))), lim, hd, b))
```

lemma `tlsf_regions_append`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (p : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  tlsf_regions(p, APPEND(ys, zs), hd)
    == star(tlsf_regions(p, ys, hd), tlsf_regions(tlsf_end(p, ys), zs, hd))
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_cut`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd) == (
    ∃ b : Num,
      tlsf_path(ys, a, lim, hd, b)
      && tlsf_layout(tlsf_end(p, ys), b, lim, top, CONS(m, zs), hd))
```

lemma `tlsf_regions_cong`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p : W32) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. ∀ m : W32, MEM(m, xs) → hd(m) == nh(m)
then
  tlsf_regions(p, xs, hd) == tlsf_regions(p, xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_cong`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. ∀ m : W32, MEM(m, xs) → hd(m) == nh(m)
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd) == tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_path_cong`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (a lim b : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. ∀ m : W32, MEM(m, xs) → hd(m) == nh(m)
then
  tlsf_path(xs, a, lim, hd, b) == tlsf_path(xs, a, lim, nh, b)
```

definition `tlsf_proved_spec`

```
tlsf_proved_spec(
  (addr : Num),
  (code : List[B_e]),
  (zs : List[Value]),
  (nin : Num),
  (nout : Num),
  (a : Num),
  (P : FProp),
  (Q : FProp)) == (
  a == addr
  && (
       ∀ args : List[Value],
         sl_triple(
           (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
           (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
             vs == NIL && lc == APPEND(args, zs) && P(args, gs, ms, h),
           code,
           (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
           (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
             Q(vs, gs, ms, h)))
  && (
       ∀ args : List[Value],
         ∀ gs : List[Value],
           ∀ ms : Num, ∀ h : Heap, P(args, gs, ms, h) → LENGTH(args) == nin)
  && (
       ∀ res : List[Value],
         ∀ gs : List[Value],
           ∀ ms : Num, ∀ h : Heap, Q(res, gs, ms, h) → LENGTH(res) == nout))
```

lemma `tlsf_proved_specs`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (addr n : Num) (code : List[B_e])
  (zs : List[Value]) (ts ins outs : List[Valtype])
if
  1. EL(addr, fs) == Func_native(inst, Tf(ins, outs), ts, code)
  2. n_zeros(ts) == SOME(zs)
  3. call_free_b(code)
then
  specs_ok(
    n,
    tlsf_proved_spec(addr, code, zs, LENGTH(ins), LENGTH(outs)),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst)
```

definition `tlsf_put`

```
tlsf_put((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (v : W32), (x : W32))
  == (if x == m { v } else { hd(x) })
```

definition `tlsf_mark`

```
tlsf_mark((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32))
  == tlsf_put(hd, m, word_or(word_and(hd(m), word(4294967294)), word(1)))
```

definition `tlsf_remainder`

```
tlsf_remainder((m : W32), (n : W32)) == word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8)))
```

definition `tlsf_remainder_size`

```
tlsf_remainder_size((h : W32), (n : W32))
  == word_sub(word_sub(tlsf_size(h), n), word(8))
```

definition `tlsf_split_headers`

```
tlsf_split_headers((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (n : W32)) == tlsf_put(
  tlsf_put(hd, m, word_or(n, word(1))),
  tlsf_remainder(m, n),
  word_or(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), word_and(hd(m), word(2))))
```

definition `tlsf_bin_add`

```
tlsf_bin_add(
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (f : Num),
  (j : Num),
  (m : W32),
  (i : Num),
  (k : Num))
  == (if i == f && k == j { CONS(m, bl(i, k)) } else { bl(i, k) })
```

lemma `tlsf_size_mask_id`

```
∀ (h : W32), tlsf_size(tlsf_size(h)) == tlsf_size(h)
```

lemma `tlsf_size_value`

```
∀ (h : W32), val(tlsf_size(h)) == 4 * DIV(val(h), 4)
```

lemma `tlsf_size_clean`

```
∀ (h : W32), (tlsf_size(h) == h) == (MOD(val(h), 4) == 0)
```

lemma `tlsf_remainder_mask`

```
∀ (h n : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(n) == n
  2. val(n) + 8 <= val(tlsf_size(h))
then
  tlsf_size(tlsf_remainder_size(h, n)) == tlsf_remainder_size(h, n)
```

lemma `tlsf_header_fields_clean`

```
∀ (h : W32) (last used : Bool)
if
  1. tlsf_size(h) == h
then
  tlsf_size(tlsf_header(h, last, used)) == h
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_header(h, last, used)) == last
  && tlsf_used(tlsf_header(h, last, used)) == used
```

lemma `tlsf_class_size`

```
∀ (h v : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(h) == tlsf_size(v)
then
  tlsf_level_of(h) == tlsf_level_of(v)
  && tlsf_slot_of(h) == tlsf_slot_of(v)
```

lemma `tlsf_count_cons`

```
∀ (m a : W32) (xs : List[W32]),
  tlsf_count(m, CONS(a, xs))
    == (if m == a { SUC(tlsf_count(m, xs)) } else { tlsf_count(m, xs) })
```

definition `tlsf_push_fl`

```
tlsf_push_fl((hd : W32), (fl : W32)) == word_or(
  fl,
  word_jshl(word(1), word_sub(word(27), word(word_clz(tlsf_size(hd))))))
```

definition `tlsf_push_sb`

```
tlsf_push_sb((hd : W32), (sb : (Num) -> W32)) == (
  tlsf_upd : ((Num) -> W32, Num, W32, Num) -> W32)(
  sb,
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
      (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
  (word_or : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
    sb(
      (val : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
          (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd)))))),
    (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(1),
      (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
        (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          tlsf_size(hd),
          (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
        (word : (Num) -> W32)(15)))))
```

definition `tlsf_push_bl`

```
tlsf_push_bl((hd : W32), (m : W32), (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  tlsf_upd : ((Num, Num) -> List[W32], Num, (Num) -> List[W32], Num, Num) -> List[
    W32])(
  bl,
  (val : (W32) -> Num)(
    (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
      (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
  (tlsf_upd : ((Num) -> List[W32], Num, List[W32], Num) -> List[W32])(
    bl(
      (val : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
          (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd)))))),
    (val : (W32) -> Num)(
      (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
        (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          tlsf_size(hd),
          (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
        (word : (Num) -> W32)(15))),
    (CONS : (W32, List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
      m,
      bl(
        (val : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
        (val : (W32) -> Num)(
          (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
              tlsf_size(hd),
              (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
                (word : (Num) -> W32)(
                  (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(15)))))))
```

definition `tlsf_push_pre`

```
tlsf_push_pre(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : W32),
  (pf0 : W32),
  (nf0 : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (args : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    args == [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]
    && (
         (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
           star(
             pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
             star(
               pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
               star(
                 pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
                 star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms))))))(gs, ms, h))
```

definition `tlsf_push_post`

```
tlsf_push_post(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (res : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    res == [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))]
    && (
         (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
           star(
             pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
             star(
               tlsf_bins(
                 d,
                 tlsf_push_fl(hd, fl),
                 tlsf_push_sb(hd, sb),
                 tlsf_push_bl(hd, m, bl)),
               fp(gs, ms))))(gs, ms, h))
```

lemma `tlsf_push_call`

```
∀ (d m hd pf0 nf0 fl : W32) (addr : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
  2. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
then
  tlsf_proved_spec(
    addr,
    tlsf_push_code(d),
    [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
     V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
    1,
    1,
    addr,
    tlsf_push_pre(d, m, hd, pf0, nf0, fl, sb, bl, fp),
    tlsf_push_post(d, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp))
```

example `tlsf_split_no_remainder`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd : W32)
  (push : Num) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !word_ugt(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)), word_add(n, word(264)))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)), fp(gs, ms))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && (
           (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
             star(
               pts(
                 val(m),
                 serialise_i32(
                   word_or(word_and(hd, word(4294967294)), word(1)))),
               fp(gs, ms)))(gs, ms, h),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_split_head`

```
∀ (zs : List[W32]) (p top m r : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, CONS(m, zs), hd)
  2. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  3. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  4. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(r)))
  5. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(r))), 8) == 0
  6. val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  7. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(r))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  8. !tlsf_last(nh(m))
  9. tlsf_last(nh(r)) == tlsf_last(hd(m))
  10. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_layout(
    p,
    a,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { r } else { top },
    CONS(m, CONS(r, zs)),
    nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_replace_head`

```
∀ (zs : List[W32]) (p top m : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, CONS(m, zs), hd)
  2. tlsf_size(nh(m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_last(nh(m)) == tlsf_last(hd(m))
  4. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, CONS(m, zs), nh)
```

example `tlsf_split_last_raw`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd w0 w1 pf nf fl : W32)
  (push : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. word_ugt(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)), word_add(n, word(264)))
  2. !(word_and(hd, word(2)) == word(0))
  3. !(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))) == word(0))
  4. 16 <= val(
       tlsf_size(
         word_and(
           word_or(
             word_sub(word_sub(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)), n), word(8)),
             word_and(w0, word(3))),
           word(4294967294))))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    tlsf_proved_spec(
      sfunc_ind(inst, push),
      tlsf_push_code(d),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      1,
      1),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8)))), serialise_i32(w0)),
            star(
              pts(
                val(word_add(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))), word(4))),
                serialise_i32(w1)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(
                    word_add(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))), word(8))),
                  serialise_i32(pf)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                        word(12))),
                    serialise_i32(nf)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(1908))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms))))))))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && (
           (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
             star(
               pts(
                 val(m),
                 serialise_i32(
                   word_or(
                     word_and(
                       word_or(
                         n,
                         word_and(
                           word_and(hd, word(4294967293)),
                           word(3))),
                       word(4294967294)),
                     word(1)))),
               star(
                 pts(
                   val(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8)))),
                   serialise_i32(
                     word_or(
                       word_and(
                         word_and(
                           word_or(
                             word_sub(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                 n),
                               word(8)),
                             word_and(w0, word(3))),
                           word(4294967294)),
                         word(4294967293)),
                       word(2)))),
                 star(
                   pts(
                     val(
                       word_add(
                         word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                         word(4))),
                     serialise_i32(m)),
                   star(
                     pts(
                       val(
                         word_add(
                           word_and(
                             word_add(d, word(15)),
                             word(4294967280)),
                           word(1908))),
                       serialise_i32(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))))),
                     star(
                       tlsf_bins(
                         d,
                         tlsf_push_fl(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           fl),
                         tlsf_push_sb(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           sb),
                         tlsf_push_bl(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                           bl)),
                       fp(gs, ms)))))))(gs, ms, h),
    NIL)
```

example `tlsf_split_next_raw`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd w0 w1 pf nf fl : W32)
  (push : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. word_ugt(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)), word_add(n, word(264)))
  2. word_and(hd, word(2)) == word(0)
  3. !(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))) == word(0))
  4. 16 <= val(
       tlsf_size(
         word_and(
           word_or(
             word_sub(word_sub(word_and(hd, word(4294967292)), n), word(8)),
             word_and(w0, word(3))),
           word(4294967294))))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    tlsf_proved_spec(
      sfunc_ind(inst, push),
      tlsf_push_code(d),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      1,
      1),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8)))), serialise_i32(w0)),
            star(
              pts(
                val(word_add(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))), word(4))),
                serialise_i32(w1)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(
                    word_add(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))), word(8))),
                  serialise_i32(pf)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(
                      word_add(
                        word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                        word(12))),
                    serialise_i32(nf)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(
                              m,
                              word_and(hd, word(4294967292))),
                            word(8)),
                          word(4))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms))))))))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && (
           (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
             star(
               pts(
                 val(m),
                 serialise_i32(
                   word_or(
                     word_and(
                       word_or(n, word_and(hd, word(3))),
                       word(4294967294)),
                     word(1)))),
               star(
                 pts(
                   val(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8)))),
                   serialise_i32(
                     word_and(
                       word_and(
                         word_or(
                           word_sub(
                             word_sub(
                               word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                               n),
                             word(8)),
                           word_and(w0, word(3))),
                         word(4294967294)),
                       word(4294967293)))),
                 star(
                   pts(
                     val(
                       word_add(
                         word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                         word(4))),
                     serialise_i32(m)),
                   star(
                     pts(
                       val(
                         word_add(
                           word_add(
                             word_add(
                               m,
                               word_and(hd, word(4294967292))),
                             word(8)),
                           word(4))),
                       serialise_i32(word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))))),
                     star(
                       tlsf_bins(
                         d,
                         tlsf_push_fl(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           fl),
                         tlsf_push_sb(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           sb),
                         tlsf_push_bl(
                           word_and(
                             word_or(
                               word_sub(
                                 word_sub(
                                   word_and(hd, word(4294967292)),
                                   n),
                                 word(8)),
                               word_and(w0, word(3))),
                             word(4294967294)),
                           word_add(n, word_add(m, word(8))),
                           bl)),
                       fp(gs, ms)))))))(gs, ms, h),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_coalesced_mono`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_coalesced(xs, hd)
  2. ∀ m : W32, MEM(m, xs) && tlsf_used(hd(m)) → tlsf_used(nh(m))
then
  tlsf_coalesced(xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_hd_splice`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r : W32),
  HD(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs)))) == HD(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)))
```

lemma `tlsf_coalesced_splice`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. zs == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(HD(zs)))
then
  tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_interior`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top m r : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
  3. val(m) < val(r)
  4. val(r) < val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
then
  !MEM(r, xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_transport`

```
∀ (xs ys : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. ∀ m : W32,
       (MEM(m, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(m))) == (MEM(m, ys) && !tlsf_used(nh(m)))
  2. ∀ m : W32,
       MEM(m, xs)
       && !tlsf_used(hd(m)) → tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_level_of(nh(m))
       && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_slot_of(nh(m))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl) == tlsf_bins_match(ys, nh, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_bin_add_mem`

```
∀ (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (f j i k : Num) (r m : W32),
  MEM(m, tlsf_bin_add(bl, f, j, r, i, k))
    == (MEM(m, bl(i, k)) || i == f && k == j && m == r)
```

lemma `tlsf_bin_add_count_other`

```
∀ (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (f j i k : Num) (r m : W32)
if
  1. !(m == r)
then
  tlsf_count(m, tlsf_bin_add(bl, f, j, r, i, k)) == tlsf_count(m, bl(i, k))
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_insert`

```
∀ (xs ys : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (r : W32) (f j : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. !MEM(r, xs)
  3. f < 28
  4. j < 16
  5. MEM(r, ys)
  6. !tlsf_used(nh(r))
  7. tlsf_level_of(nh(r)) == f
  8. tlsf_slot_of(nh(r)) == j
  9. ∀ m : W32,
       !(m == r) → (MEM(m, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(m)))
         == (MEM(m, ys) && !tlsf_used(nh(m)))
       && (
            MEM(m, xs)
            && !tlsf_used(hd(m)) → tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_level_of(nh(m))
            && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_slot_of(nh(m)))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(ys, nh, tlsf_bin_add(bl, f, j, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_mem_hd`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32])
if
  1. !(xs == NIL)
then
  MEM(HD(xs), xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_coalesced_suffix`

```
∀ (ys xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, xs), hd)
then
  tlsf_coalesced(xs, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_cut_at`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
then
  tlsf_path(ys, a, lim, hd, val(m))
  && tlsf_layout(tlsf_end(p, ys), val(m), lim, top, CONS(m, zs), hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_glue`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m p top : W32) (a b lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_path(ys, a, lim, hd, b)
  2. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  3. tlsf_layout(tlsf_end(p, ys), b, lim, top, CONS(m, zs), nh)
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_physical_split`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_used(nh(m))
  4. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  5. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  6. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  7. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  8. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(r)))
  9. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(r))), 8) == 0
  10. val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  11. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(r))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  12. !tlsf_last(nh(m))
  13. tlsf_last(nh(r)) == tlsf_last(hd(m))
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(
    d,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { r } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))),
    nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_size_low_bits`

```
∀ (h : W32),
  word_and(tlsf_size(h), word(1)) == word(0)
  && word_and(tlsf_size(h), word(2)) == word(0)
  && word_and(tlsf_size(h), word(4294967294)) == tlsf_size(h)
  && word_and(tlsf_size(h), word(4294967293)) == tlsf_size(h)
```

lemma `tlsf_clean_low_bits`

```
∀ (n : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(n) == n
then
  word_and(n, word(1)) == word(0)
  && word_and(n, word(2)) == word(0)
  && word_and(n, word(4294967294)) == n
  && word_and(n, word(4294967293)) == n
```

lemma `tlsf_last_mask_bit`

```
∀ (h : W32), word_and(h, word(2)) == (if bit(1, h) { word(2) } else { word(0) })
```

lemma `tlsf_last_mask_cases`

```
∀ (h : W32), word_and(h, word(2)) == word(0) || word_and(h, word(2)) == word(2)
```

lemma `tlsf_used_store_fields`

```
∀ (h : W32),
  tlsf_size(word_or(word_and(h, word(4294967294)), word(1))) == tlsf_size(h)
  && tlsf_last(word_or(word_and(h, word(4294967294)), word(1))) == tlsf_last(h)
  && tlsf_used(word_or(word_and(h, word(4294967294)), word(1)))
```

lemma `tlsf_taken_fields`

```
∀ (n : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(n) == n
then
  tlsf_size(word_or(n, word(1))) == n
  && !tlsf_last(word_or(n, word(1)))
  && tlsf_used(word_or(n, word(1)))
```

lemma `tlsf_remainder_fields`

```
∀ (s h : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(s) == s
then
  tlsf_size(word_or(s, word_and(h, word(2)))) == s
  && tlsf_last(word_or(s, word_and(h, word(2)))) == tlsf_last(h)
  && !tlsf_used(word_or(s, word_and(h, word(2))))
```

lemma `tlsf_remainder_seed_size`

```
∀ (s old : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(s) == s
then
  tlsf_size(word_and(word_or(s, word_and(old, word(3))), word(4294967294))) == s
```

lemma `tlsf_remainder_seed_clear`

```
∀ (s old : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(s) == s
then
  word_and(
    word_and(word_or(s, word_and(old, word(3))), word(4294967294)),
    word(4294967293))
    == s
```

lemma `tlsf_split_taken_last`

```
∀ (n h : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(n) == n
then
  word_or(
    word_and(
      word_or(n, word_and(word_and(h, word(4294967293)), word(3))),
      word(4294967294)),
    word(1))
    == word_or(n, word(1))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_taken_next`

```
∀ (n h : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(n) == n
  2. word_and(h, word(2)) == word(0)
then
  word_or(word_and(word_or(n, word_and(h, word(3))), word(4294967294)), word(1))
    == word_or(n, word(1))
```

lemma `tlsf_mod16_8`

```
∀ (x : Num)
if
  1. MOD(x, 16) == 0
then
  MOD(x, 8) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_mod8_4`

```
∀ (x : Num)
if
  1. MOD(x, 8) == 0
then
  MOD(x, 4) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_val_add_exact`

```
∀ (x y : W32)
if
  1. val(x) + val(y) < 4294967296
then
  val(word_add(x, y)) == val(x) + val(y)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_test`

```
∀ (h n : W32)
if
  1. val(n) <= 4160749568
then
  word_ugt(tlsf_size(h), word_add(n, word(264)))
    == (val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_aligned_sub`

```
∀ (z n : Num)
if
  1. MOD(z, 8) == 0
  2. MOD(n, 8) == 0
  3. n + 8 <= z
then
  MOD(z - n - 8, 8) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_remainder_value`

```
∀ (h n : W32)
if
  1. val(n) + 8 <= val(tlsf_size(h))
then
  val(tlsf_remainder_size(h, n)) == val(tlsf_size(h)) - val(n) - 8
```

lemma `tlsf_split_bounds`

```
∀ (m n : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (lim : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_block_ok(val(m), lim, m, hd)
  2. lim <= 4294967296
  3. 16 <= val(n)
  4. val(n) <= 4160749568
  5. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  6. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
then
  val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)) == val(m) + 8 + val(n)
  && !(tlsf_remainder(m, n) == word(0))
  && !(tlsf_remainder(m, n) == m)
  && MOD(val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)), 8) == 0
  && val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) - val(n) - 8
  && 16 <= val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n))
  && MOD(val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)), 8) == 0
  && tlsf_size(n) == n
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)
  && val(n) + 8 + val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
```

lemma `tlsf_byte_div`

```
∀ (h : Byte) (n : Num), DIV(val(h) + 256 * n, 256) == n
```

lemma `tlsf_le_bytes_num`

```
∀ (bs : List[Byte]), le_bytes(LENGTH(bs), num_of_bytes(bs)) == bs
```

lemma `tlsf_le_bytes_length`

```
∀ (k n : Num), LENGTH(le_bytes(k, n)) == k
```

lemma `tlsf_le_bytes_mod`

```
∀ (k n : Num), le_bytes(k, MOD(n, EXP(256, k))) == le_bytes(k, n)
```

lemma `tlsf_serialise_deserialise`

```
∀ (bs : List[Byte])
if
  1. LENGTH(bs) == 4
then
  serialise_i32(deserialise_i32(bs)) == bs
```

lemma `tlsf_bytes_word`

```
∀ (a : Num),
  tlsf_bytes(a, 4) == ((h : Heap) => ∃ v : W32, pts(a, serialise_i32(v), h))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_drop`

```
∀ (xs : List[Byte]) (n : Num), APPEND(take(n, xs), drop(n, xs)) == xs
```

lemma `tlsf_bytes_split`

```
∀ (a n k : Num),
  tlsf_bytes(a, n + k) == star(tlsf_bytes(a, n), tlsf_bytes(a + n, k))
```

lemma `tlsf_bytes_four_words`

```
∀ (a : Num),
  tlsf_bytes(a, 16) == (
    (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ w0 : W32,
        ∃ w1 : W32,
          ∃ pf : W32,
            ∃ nf : W32,
              star(
                pts(a, serialise_i32(w0)),
                star(
                  pts(a + 4, serialise_i32(w1)),
                  star(
                    pts(a + 8, serialise_i32(pf)),
                    pts(a + 12, serialise_i32(nf)))),
                h))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_payload_cut`

```
∀ (a n s z : Num)
if
  1. 16 <= s
  2. n + 8 + s == z
then
  tlsf_bytes(a, z) == star(
    tlsf_bytes(a, n),
    star(tlsf_bytes(a + n, 16), tlsf_bytes(a + n + 16, s - 8)))
```

lemma `tlsf_push_call_free`

```
∀ (d : W32), call_free_b(tlsf_push_code(d))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_in_module`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d : W32) (push : Num)
  (P Q : SAssn)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. sl_triple_g(
       tlsf_proved_spec(
         sfunc_ind(inst, push),
         tlsf_push_code(d),
         [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
          V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
         1,
         1),
       fs,
       tabs,
       inst,
       (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
       (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
         false,
       P,
       tlsf_split_code(d, push),
       Q,
       NIL)
then
  sl_triple_m(fs, tabs, inst, P, tlsf_split_code(d, push), Q, NIL)
```

lemma `sl_ex_m`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst)
  (P : (A, List[Value], List[Value], List[Value], Num, Heap) -> Bool)
  (code : List[B_e]) (Q : SAssn) (labels : List[SAssn])
if
  1. ∀ x : A, sl_triple_m(fs, tabs, inst, P(x), code, Q, labels)
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ x : A, P(x, vs, lc, gs, ms, h),
    code,
    Q,
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_physical_mark`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  4. tlsf_size(nh(m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  5. tlsf_last(nh(m)) == tlsf_last(hd(m))
  6. tlsf_used(nh(m))
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_focus_distinct`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
then
  !MEM(m, ys)
  && !MEM(m, zs)
```

lemma `tlsf_mark_focus`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m))
  && tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, tlsf_mark(hd, m))
       == tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd)
  && tlsf_regions(m, zs, tlsf_mark(hd, m)) == tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd)
  && tlsf_mark(hd, m, m) == word_or(word_and(hd(m), word(4294967294)), word(1))
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_mark(hd, m, m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  && tlsf_used(tlsf_mark(hd, m, m))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_focus_geometry`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m n d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. 16 <= val(n)
  4. val(n) <= 4160749568
  5. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  6. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
then
  val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)) == val(m) + 8 + val(n)
  && !(tlsf_remainder(m, n) == word(0))
  && !(tlsf_remainder(m, n) == m)
  && val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) - val(n) - 8
  && 16 <= val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n))
  && MOD(val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)), 8) == 0
  && tlsf_size(n) == n
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)
  && val(n) + 8 + val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  && !MEM(tlsf_remainder(m, n), APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)))
  && !MEM(m, ys)
  && !MEM(m, zs)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_focus`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m n d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. 16 <= val(n)
  4. val(n) <= 4160749568
  5. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  6. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(
    d,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { tlsf_remainder(m, n) } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs))),
    tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n))
  && ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, x)
  && ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, x)
  && tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, m) == word_or(n, word(1))
  && tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, tlsf_remainder(m, n))
       == word_or(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), word_and(hd(m), word(2)))
```

lemma `tlsf_push_bl_add`

```
∀ (h r : W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  tlsf_push_bl(h, r, bl)
    == tlsf_bin_add(bl, tlsf_level_of(h), tlsf_slot_of(h), r)
```

lemma `tlsf_push_size_cong`

```
∀ (h v r fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_size(h) == tlsf_size(v)
then
  tlsf_push_fl(h, fl) == tlsf_push_fl(v, fl)
  && tlsf_push_sb(h, sb) == tlsf_push_sb(v, sb)
  && tlsf_push_bl(h, r, bl) == tlsf_push_bl(v, r, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_bins_focus`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m n : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  2. !MEM(tlsf_remainder(m, n), APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)))
  3. !(tlsf_remainder(m, n) == m)
  4. tlsf_size(n) == n
  5. tlsf_size(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)
  6. 16 <= val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs))),
    tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n),
    tlsf_push_bl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), tlsf_remainder(m, n), bl))
```

lemma `tlsf_mark_close`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top fl : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
then
  star(
    pts(val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))), serialise_i32(top)),
    star(
      tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
      star(
        pts(
          val(m),
          serialise_i32(word_or(word_and(hd(m), word(4294967294)), word(1)))),
        star(
          pts(
            val(word_add(m, word(4))),
            serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
          star(
            tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
            star(
              tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd),
              star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp))))))) == star(
    tlsf_large(
      d,
      lim,
      top,
      APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
      tlsf_mark(hd, m),
      fl,
      sb,
      bl),
    star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_close`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m n d top fl : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(
       d,
       lim,
       if zs == NIL { tlsf_remainder(m, n) } else { top },
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs))),
       tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n))
  2. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs))),
       tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n),
       tlsf_push_bl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), tlsf_remainder(m, n), bl))
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, x)
  4. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, x)
  5. tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, m) == word_or(n, word(1))
  6. tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n, tlsf_remainder(m, n))
       == word_or(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), word_and(hd(m), word(2)))
  7. tlsf_size(n) == n
  8. tlsf_size(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) == tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)
then
  star(
    pts(
      val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))),
      serialise_i32(if zs == NIL { tlsf_remainder(m, n) } else { top })),
    star(
      tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
      star(
        pts(val(m), serialise_i32(word_or(n, word(1)))),
        star(
          pts(
            val(word_add(m, word(4))),
            serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
          star(
            pts(
              val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)),
              serialise_i32(
                word_or(
                  tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n),
                  word_and(hd(m), word(2))))),
            star(
              pts(
                val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(4))),
                serialise_i32(m)),
              star(
                tlsf_bytes(
                  val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)) + 16,
                  val(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n)) - 8),
                star(
                  tlsf_regions(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs, hd),
                  star(
                    tlsf_bins(
                      d,
                      tlsf_push_fl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), fl),
                      tlsf_push_sb(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), sb),
                      tlsf_push_bl(
                        tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n),
                        tlsf_remainder(m, n),
                        bl)),
                    star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(n)), fp)))))))))) == star(
    tlsf_large(
      d,
      lim,
      if zs == NIL { tlsf_remainder(m, n) } else { top },
      APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(tlsf_remainder(m, n), zs))),
      tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n),
      tlsf_push_fl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), fl),
      tlsf_push_sb(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), sb),
      tlsf_push_bl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), tlsf_remainder(m, n), bl)),
    star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(n)), fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_split_whole`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  5. 16 <= val(n)
  6. val(n) <= 4160749568
  7. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  8. val(n) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  9. !word_ugt(tlsf_size(hd(m)), word_add(n, word(264)))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
          (h : Heap) =>
            ms == lim
            && star(
                 pts(
                   val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))),
                   serialise_i32(top)),
                 star(
                   tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
                   star(
                     pts(
                       val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                       serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
                     star(
                       tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
                       star(
                         tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd),
                         star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))))),
                 h))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && ms == lim
      && star(
           tlsf_large(
             d,
             lim,
             top,
             APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
             tlsf_mark(hd, m),
             fl,
             sb,
             bl),
           star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp(gs, ms)),
           h),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_last`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top w0 w1 pf nf fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. 16 <= val(n)
  5. val(n) <= 4160749568
  6. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  7. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  8. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  9. !(word_and(hd(m), word(2)) == word(0))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
          star(
            pts(val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)), serialise_i32(w0)),
            star(
              pts(
                val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(4))),
                serialise_i32(w1)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(8))),
                  serialise_i32(pf)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(12))),
                    serialise_i32(nf)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_and(
                            word_add(d, word(15)),
                            word(4294967280)),
                          word(1908))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    star(
                      tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl),
                      (h : Heap) =>
                        ms == lim
                        && star(
                             tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
                             star(
                               pts(
                                 val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                                 serialise_i32(
                                   tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
                               star(
                                 tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(n)),
                                 star(
                                   tlsf_bytes(
                                     val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)) + 16,
                                     val(
                                       tlsf_remainder_size(
                                         hd(m),
                                         n)) - 8),
                                   fp(gs, ms)))),
                             h))))))))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && ms == lim
      && star(
           tlsf_large(
             d,
             lim,
             tlsf_remainder(m, n),
             APPEND(ys, [m, tlsf_remainder(m, n)]),
             tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n),
             tlsf_push_fl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), fl),
             tlsf_push_sb(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), sb),
             tlsf_push_bl(
               tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n),
               tlsf_remainder(m, n),
               bl)),
           star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(n)), fp(gs, ms)),
           h),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_next`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top w0 w1 pf nf fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(
       d,
       lim,
       top,
       APPEND(
         ys,
         CONS(m, CONS(word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8)), zs))),
       hd)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. 16 <= val(n)
  5. val(n) <= 4160749568
  6. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  7. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  8. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(
         ys,
         CONS(m, CONS(word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8)), zs))),
       tlsf_mark(hd, m),
       bl)
  9. word_and(hd(m), word(2)) == word(0)
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
          star(
            pts(val(tlsf_remainder(m, n)), serialise_i32(w0)),
            star(
              pts(
                val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(4))),
                serialise_i32(w1)),
              star(
                pts(
                  val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(8))),
                  serialise_i32(pf)),
                star(
                  pts(
                    val(word_add(tlsf_remainder(m, n), word(12))),
                    serialise_i32(nf)),
                  star(
                    pts(
                      val(
                        word_add(
                          word_add(
                            word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))),
                            word(8)),
                          word(4))),
                      serialise_i32(m)),
                    star(
                      tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl),
                      (h : Heap) =>
                        ms == lim
                        && star(
                             tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
                             star(
                               pts(
                                 val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                                 serialise_i32(
                                   tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
                               star(

                                 star(

                                         n)) - 8),
                                   star(

                                       star(

                                             word(8)),
                                           hd(

                                               word_add(
                                                 m,

                                                   hd(m))),
                                               word(8)))),
                                         star(

                                               word_add(
                                                 m,
                                                 tlsf_size(
                                                   hd(m))),
                                               word(8)),
                                             zs,
                                             hd),
                                           fp(gs, ms)))))))),
                             h))))))))),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))

                     zs)))),

             tlsf_push_fl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), fl),
             tlsf_push_sb(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), sb),
             tlsf_push_bl(

lemma `tlsf_split_next_all`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(
       d,
       lim,
       top,
       APPEND(
         ys,
         CONS(m, CONS(word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8)), zs))),
       hd)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. 16 <= val(n)
  5. val(n) <= 4160749568
  6. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  7. val(n) + 264 < val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  8. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(
         ys,
         CONS(m, CONS(word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8)), zs))),
       tlsf_mark(hd, m),
       bl)
  9. word_and(hd(m), word(2)) == word(0)
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
             star(
               pts(
                 val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))),
                 serialise_i32(top)),
               star(
                 tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
                 star(
                   pts(
                     val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                     serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
                   star(
                     tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
                     star(
                       tlsf_regions(
                         m,
                         CONS(
                           word_add(
                             word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))),
                             word(8)),
                           zs),
                         hd),
                       star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms))))))),
             h)),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && ms == lim
      && star(
           tlsf_large(
             d,
             lim,
             top,
             APPEND(
               ys,
               CONS(
                 m,
                 CONS(
                   tlsf_remainder(m, n),
                   CONS(
                     word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8)),
                     zs)))),
             tlsf_split_headers(hd, m, n),
             tlsf_push_fl(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), fl),
             tlsf_push_sb(tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n), sb),
             tlsf_push_bl(
               tlsf_remainder_size(hd(m), n),
               tlsf_remainder(m, n),
               bl)),
           star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(n)), fp(gs, ms)),
           h),
    NIL)
```

definition `tlsf_detached`

```
tlsf_detached(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (ys : List[W32]),
  (m : W32),
  (zs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
    && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
    && star(
         pts(val(word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))), serialise_i32(top)),
         star(
           tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
           star(
             pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
             star(
               pts(
                 val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                 serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
               star(
                 tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
                 star(tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd), tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl)))))),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_allocated`

```
tlsf_allocated((d : W32), (lim : Num), (m : W32), (n : W32), (client : HProp)) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    !(m == word(0))
    && (
         ∃ top2 : W32,
           ∃ xs2 : List[W32],
             ∃ hd2 : (W32) -> W32,
               ∃ fl2 : W32,
                 ∃ sb2 : (Num) -> W32,
                   ∃ bl2 : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                     MEM(m, xs2)
                     && tlsf_used(hd2(m))
                     && val(n) <= val(tlsf_size(hd2(m)))
                     && star(
                          tlsf_large(d, lim, top2, xs2, hd2, fl2, sb2, bl2),
                          star(
                            tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd2(m)))),
                            client),
                          h)))
```

    && 16 <= val(s)

```
tlsf_take_post(
  (d : W32),
  (s : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),

lemma `tlsf_take_code_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_take_code(d, pop, push)
    == APPEND(tlsf_find_code(d), tlsf_take_tail(d, pop, push))

lemma `tlsf_align_mod`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  MOD(val(tlsf_align(s)), 16) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_align_fixed`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. val(s) <= 4160749568
  2. MOD(val(s), 16) == 0
then
  tlsf_align(s) == s
```

lemma `tlsf_align_idem`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  tlsf_align(tlsf_align(s)) == tlsf_align(s)
```

lemma `tlsf_search_align`

```
∀ (s fl : W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  tlsf_search(tlsf_align(s), fl, sb, bl) == tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl)
```
