```
    (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
      (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
      (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
  (tlsf_upd : ((Num) -> List[W32], Num, List[W32], Num) -> List[W32])(
    bl(
      (val : (W32) -> Num)(
        (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
          (word : (Num) -> W32)((word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd)))))),
    (val : (W32) -> Num)(
      (word_and : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
        (word_jushr : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
          tlsf_size(hd),
          (word_sub : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(27),
            (word : (Num) -> W32)(
              (word_clz : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_size(hd))))),
        (word : (Num) -> W32)(15))),
    xs))
```

lemma `tlsf_mark_other`

```
∀ (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m x : W32)
if
  1. !(x == m)
then
  tlsf_mark(hd, m, x) == hd(x)
```

lemma `tlsf_mark_used`

```
∀ (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m : W32), tlsf_used(tlsf_mark(hd, m, m))
```

lemma `tlsf_count_append`

```
∀ (xs ys : List[W32]) (m : W32),
  tlsf_count(m, APPEND(xs, ys)) == tlsf_count(m, xs) + tlsf_count(m, ys)
```

lemma `tlsf_remove_other_count`

```
∀ (us vs : List[W32]) (m x : W32)
if
  1. !(x == m)
then
  tlsf_count(x, APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))) == tlsf_count(x, APPEND(us, vs))
```

lemma `tlsf_remove_self_count`

```
∀ (us vs : List[W32]) (m : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_count(m, APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))) == 1
then
  tlsf_count(m, APPEND(us, vs)) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_bl_other_count`

```
∀ (hd m x : W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (us vs : List[W32]) (i k : Num)
if
  1. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
  2. !(x == m)
then
  tlsf_count(x, tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs), i, k))
    == tlsf_count(x, bl(i, k))
```

lemma `sl_triple_g_of_m_closed`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp P Q : SAssn)
  (code : List[B_e]) (labels : List[SAssn])
if
  1. sl_triple_m(fs, tabs, inst, P, code, Q, NIL)
then
  sl_triple_g(G, fs, tabs, inst, rp, P, code, Q, labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_locals_six`

```
∀ (lc : List[Value])
if
  1. LENGTH(lc) == 6
then
  lc == [EL(0, lc), EL(1, lc), EL(2, lc), EL(3, lc), EL(4, lc), EL(5, lc)]
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_detach`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl nb : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (m : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. ∀ x : W32,
       ∀ i : Num,
         ∀ j : Num,
           !(x == m) → tlsf_count(x, nb(i, j)) == tlsf_count(x, bl(i, j))
  3. ∀ i : Num, ∀ j : Num, i < 28 && j < 16 → tlsf_count(m, nb(i, j)) == 0
then
  tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m), nb)
```

definition `tlsf_pop_pre`

```
tlsf_pop_pre(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (us : List[W32]),
  (vs : List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (args : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    args == [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]
    && star(
         pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
         star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_pop_post`

```
tlsf_pop_post(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (us : List[W32]),
  (vs : List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (res : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    res == NIL
    && star(
         pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
         star(
           pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
           star(
             pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
             star(
               tlsf_bins(
                 d,
                 tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                 tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                 tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
               fp(gs, ms)))),
         h))
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_call`

```
∀ (d m hd fl : W32) (addr : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (us vs : List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  2. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  tlsf_proved_spec(
    addr,
    tlsf_pop_code(d),
    [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
     V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
    1,
    0,
    addr,
    tlsf_pop_pre(d, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, us, vs, fp),
    tlsf_pop_post(d, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, us, vs, fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_call_free`

```
∀ (d : W32), call_free_b(tlsf_pop_code(d))
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_bl_at`

```
∀ (h : W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (xs : List[W32]) (i j : Num),
  tlsf_pop_bl(h, bl, xs, i, j) == (
    if i == tlsf_level_of(h) && j == tlsf_slot_of(h) { xs } else { bl(i, j) })
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_bins_match`

```
∀ (xs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (m : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m), tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs)))
```

example `tlsf_pop_invoke_g`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (us vs : List[W32])
  (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  2. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    tlsf_proved_spec(
      sfunc_ind(inst, pop),
      tlsf_pop_code(d),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      1,
      0),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(
                tlsf_bins(
                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
                fp(gs, ms)))))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_invoke`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (us vs : List[W32])
  (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  3. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(
                tlsf_bins(
                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
                fp(gs, ms)))))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_payload_links`

```
∀ (a z : Num)
if
  1. 8 <= z
then
  tlsf_bytes(a, z) == (
    (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ pf : W32,
        ∃ nf : W32,
          star(
            pts(a, serialise_i32(pf)),
            star(pts(a + 4, serialise_i32(nf)), tlsf_bytes(a + 8, z - 8)),
            h))
```

lemma `tlsf_focus_block`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
then
  tlsf_block_ok(val(m), lim, m, hd)
  && lim <= 4294967296
```

lemma `tlsf_focus_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
then
  top == m
  && tlsf_last(hd(m))
```

lemma `tlsf_focus_next`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m q d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(q, zs))), hd)
then
  q == word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8))
  && !tlsf_last(hd(m))
```

lemma `tlsf_allocated_mono`

```
∀ (d m n s : W32) (lim : Num) (client : HProp) (h : Heap)
if
  1. val(s) <= val(n)
  2. tlsf_allocated(d, lim, m, n, client, h)
then
  tlsf_allocated(d, lim, m, s, client, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_pop_detach`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top fl : W32)
  (pop lim : Num) (ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value)
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, bl)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  5. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_detached(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               zs,
               hd,
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(m), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(m), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_take_tee`

```
∀ (m n : W32) (l0 l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (R : GProp),
  sl_triple(
    st([V_num(ConstInt32(m))], [l0, l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5], R),
    [Local_tee(0)],
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      R))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_tail_success`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (m n d : W32) (l0 l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (R : GProp) (pop push : Num)
  (ls : List[SAssn])
if
  1. !(m == word(0))
  2. ∀ ls2 : List[SAssn],
       sl_triple_g(
         G,
         fs,
         tabs,
         inst,
         rp,
         st(
           NIL,
           [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
           R),
         tlsf_take_success(d, pop, push),
         (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
         (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
           false,
         ls2)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st([V_num(ConstInt32(m))], [l0, l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5], R),
    tlsf_take_tail(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    ls)
```

lemma `tlsf_take_tail_zero_raw`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (n d : W32) (l0 l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (R : GProp) (pop push : Num)
  (ls : List[SAssn]),
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [l0, l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      R),
    [
      Local_tee(0),
      If(
        Tbv(NONE),
        [
          Local_get(0),
          Call(pop),
          Local_get(0),
          Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
          EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967292))),
          Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
          Local_tee(2),
          Local_get(3),
          EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(264))),
          Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
          Relop(T_i32, Relop_i(Gt(U))),
          If(
            Tbv(NONE),
            [
              Local_get(0),
              Local_get(2),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(8))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
              Local_set(4),
              Local_get(3),
              Local_get(0),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(8))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
              Local_tee(1),
              Local_get(2),
              Local_get(3),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Sub)),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(8))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Sub)),
              Local_get(1),
              Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(3))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Or)),
              Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              Local_get(1),
              Local_get(1),
              Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967294))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
              Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              Local_get(1),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
              Local_get(0),
              Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              Local_get(1),
              Call(push),
              Drop,
              Local_get(0),
              Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(2))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(0))),
              Relop(T_i32, Relop_i(Ne)),
              Local_tee(2),
              If(
                Tbv(NONE),
                [Local_get(1), Local_get(1), Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967293))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(2))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Or)), Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
                [Local_get(1), Local_get(1), Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967293))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)), Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)]),
              Local_get(2),
              If(
                Tbv(NONE),
                [Local_get(0), Local_get(0), Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967293))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)), Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(d)),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(15))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967280))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
                 EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(1908))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)), Local_get(1),
                 Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
                [Local_get(4), EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4))),
                 Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Add)), Local_get(1),
                 Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)]),
              Local_get(0),
              Local_get(3),
              Local_get(0),
              Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
              EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(3))),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
              Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Or)),
              Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
            NIL),
          Local_get(0),
          Local_get(0),
          Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
          EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(4294967294))),
          Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(And)),
          EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(1))),
          Binop(T_i32, Binop_i(Or)),
          Store(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0),
          Local_get(0),
          Return],
        NIL),
      Local_get(0)],
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      R),
    ls)
```

lemma `tlsf_take_tail_zero`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (n d : W32) (l0 l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (R : GProp) (pop push : Num)
  (ls : List[SAssn]),
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [l0, l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      R),
    tlsf_take_tail(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      R),
    ls)
```

lemma `tlsf_split_detached`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  5. 16 <= val(n)
  6. val(n) <= 4160749568
  7. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  8. val(n) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_detached(d, lim, top, ys, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_split_code(d, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      LENGTH(lc) == 6
      && EL(0, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(m))
      && EL(3, lc) == V_num(ConstInt32(n))
      && vs == NIL
      && ms == lim
      && tlsf_allocated(d, lim, m, n, fp(gs, ms), h),
    NIL)
```

```
if
```


lemma `tlsf_take_success_body`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (Q : FProp)
  (d m n top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num) (ys zs us bs : List[W32])
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value) (fp : GProp) (ls : List[SAssn])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
     && tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
     && tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, bl)
     && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
     && bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, bs))
  2. 16 <= val(n)
  3. val(n) <= 4160749568
  4. MOD(val(n), 16) == 0
  5. val(n) <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  6. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  7. ∀ gs : List[Value],
       ∀ ms : Num,
         ∀ h : Heap,
           (
             (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
               ms == lim && tlsf_allocated(d, lim, m, n, fp(gs, ms), h))(
             gs,
             ms,
             h) → Q([V_num(ConstInt32(m))], gs, ms, h)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      Q(vs, gs, ms, h),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2, V_num(ConstInt32(n)), l4, l5],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_take_success(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    ls)
```

lemma `tlsf_large_facts_frame`

```
∀ (d top fl : W32) (lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (client : HProp) (h : Heap)
if
  1. star(tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), client, h)
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  && tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  && tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_search_aligned_sound`

```
∀ (s d top fl : W32) (lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  4. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  5. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
then
  tlsf_find_result(tlsf_align(s), xs, hd, tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_bin_member`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (m : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
then
  MEM(m, bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_allocated_post`

```
∀ (d s top fl m n : W32) (lim ms : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before h : Heap)
  (fp : GProp) (gs : List[Value])
if
  1. ms == lim
  2. tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl) == m
  3. MEM(m, xs)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  5. val(s) <= val(n)
  6. tlsf_allocated(d, lim, m, n, fp(gs, ms), h)
then
  tlsf_take_post(
    d,
    s,
    lim,
    top,
    xs,
    hd,
    fl,
    sb,
    bl,
    before,
    fp,
    [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
    gs,
    ms,
    h)
```

```

  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
  (l0 l1 l2 l4 l5 : Value)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  4. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  5. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  6. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  7. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
  8. tlsf_search(s, fl, sb, bl) == m
then

      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && h == before
        && star(tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms), h)),

        s,
        lim,
        top,
        xs,
        hd,
        fl,
        sb,
        bl,
        before,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    [
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        tlsf_take_post(
          d,
          s,
          lim,
          top,
          xs,
          hd,
          fl,
          sb,
          bl,
          before,
          fp,
          vs,
          gs,
          ms,
          h)])
```

definition `tlsf_take_spec`

```
tlsf_take_spec(
  (inst : Inst),
  (d : W32),
  (pop : Num),
  (push : Num),
  (take_idx : Num),
  (a : Num),
  (P : FProp),
  (Q : FProp)) == (
  a == sfunc_ind(inst, take_idx)
  && (
       ∃ s : W32,
         ∃ lim : Num,
           ∃ top : W32,
             ∃ xs : List[W32],
               ∃ hd : (W32) -> W32,
                 ∃ fl : W32,
                   ∃ sb : (Num) -> W32,
                     ∃ bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                       ∃ before : Heap,
                         ∃ fp : GProp,
                           P == tlsf_take_pre(
                             d,
                             s,
                             lim,
                             top,
                             xs,
                             hd,
                             fl,
                             sb,
                             bl,
                             before,
                             fp)
                           && Q == tlsf_take_post(
                                d,
                                s,
                                lim,
                                top,
                                xs,
                                hd,
                                fl,
                                sb,
                                bl,
                                before,
                                fp)))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_spec_call`

```
∀ (inst : Inst) (d s top fl : W32) (pop push take_idx lim : Num)
  (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp),
  tlsf_take_spec(
    inst,
    d,
    pop,
    push,
    take_idx,
    sfunc_ind(inst, take_idx),
    tlsf_take_pre(d, s, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, before, fp),
    tlsf_take_post(d, s, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, before, fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_take_specs`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d : W32)
  (pop push take_idx k : Num)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, take_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_take_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  specs_ok(k, tlsf_take_spec(inst, d, pop, push, take_idx), fs, tabs, inst)
```

lemma `tlsf_take_all`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d s top fl : W32)
  (pop push take_idx lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap)
  (fp : GProp) (vs0 lc0 : List[Value])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, take_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_take_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ args : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, args)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_take_pre(
             d,
             s,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             before,
             fp,
             args,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    [Call(take_idx)],
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ res : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, res)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_take_post(
             d,
             s,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             before,
             fp,
             res,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    NIL)
```

definition `tlsf_capacity_at`

```
tlsf_capacity_at((lim : Num), (m : W32))
  == word_sub(word_sub(word(lim), m), word(8))
```

definition `tlsf_top_cell`

```
tlsf_top_cell((d : W32)) == word_add(tlsf_base(d), word(1908))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_expected`

```
tlsf_ensure_expected((s : W32)) == tlsf_round(tlsf_align(s))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_pages`

```
tlsf_ensure_pages((s : W32), (h : W32)) == (
  if tlsf_used(h) {
    word_jushr(word_add(tlsf_ensure_expected(s), word(65543)), word(16)) } else {
    word_jushr(
      word_add(word_sub(tlsf_ensure_expected(s), tlsf_size(h)), word(65535)),
      word(16)) })
```

definition `tlsf_extend_headers`

```
tlsf_extend_headers((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (lim2 : Num)) == tlsf_put(
  hd,
  m,
  word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hd(m), word(3))))
```

definition `tlsf_append_headers`

```
tlsf_append_headers(
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (m : W32),
  (oldlim : Num),
  (lim2 : Num)) == tlsf_put(
  tlsf_put(hd, m, word_and(hd(m), word(4294967293))),
  word(oldlim),
  word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, word(oldlim)), word(2)))
```

definition `tlsf_grow_headers`

```
tlsf_grow_headers((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (oldlim : Num), (lim2 : Num)) == (
  if tlsf_used(hd(m)) { tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, oldlim, lim2) } else {
    tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, lim2) })
```

definition `tlsf_grow_top`

```
tlsf_grow_top((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (oldlim : Num))
  == (if tlsf_used(hd(m)) { word(oldlim) } else { m })
```

definition `tlsf_grow_chain`

```
tlsf_grow_chain(
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (m : W32),
  (oldlim : Num))
  == (if tlsf_used(hd(m)) { APPEND(xs, [word(oldlim)]) } else { xs })
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_pre`

```
tlsf_ensure_pre(
  (d : W32),
  (s : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (before : Heap),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (args : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    args == [V_num(ConstInt32(s))]
    && 16 <= val(s)
    && val(s) <= 4160749568
    && ms == lim
    && h == before
    && star(tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, lim), h))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_post`

```
tlsf_ensure_post(
  (d : W32),
  (s : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (before : Heap),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (res : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    res == [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))]
    && ms == lim
    && h == before
    && star(tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, lim), h)
    || res == [V_num(ConstInt32(word(1)))]
       && ms == lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(top))) * 65536
       && lim <= ms
       && ms <= 4294967296
       && !tlsf_used(
            tlsf_grow_headers(hd, top, lim, ms, tlsf_grow_top(hd, top, lim)))
       && val(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)) <= val(
            tlsf_size(
              tlsf_grow_headers(
                hd,
                top,
                lim,
                ms,
                tlsf_grow_top(hd, top, lim))))
       && (
            ∃ fl2 : W32,
              ∃ sb2 : (Num) -> W32,
                ∃ bl2 : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                  star(
                    tlsf_large(
                      d,
                      ms,
                      tlsf_grow_top(hd, top, lim),
                      tlsf_grow_chain(xs, hd, top, lim),
                      tlsf_grow_headers(hd, top, lim, ms),
                      fl2,
                      sb2,
                      bl2),
                    fp(gs, lim),
                    h)))
```

lemma `st_memory_size`

```
∀ (v lc0 : List[Value]) (lim : Num) (R : GProp),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      v,
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim && R(gs, ms, h)),
    [Memory_size],
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(word(DIV(lim, 65536))))]),
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim && R(gs, ms, h)))
```

lemma `st_memory_size_at`

```
∀ (v lc0 : List[Value]) (lim : Num) (R : GProp)
if
  1. ∀ gs : List[Value], ∀ ms : Num, ∀ h : Heap, R(gs, ms, h) → ms == lim
then
  sl_triple(
    st(v, lc0, R),
    [Memory_size],
    st(APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(word(DIV(lim, 65536))))]), lc0, R))
```

lemma `st_heap`

```
∀ (v l vs lc gs : List[Value]) (R : GProp) (ms : Num),
  st(v, l, R, vs, lc, gs, ms)
    == ((h : Heap) => vs == v && lc == l && R(gs, ms, h))
```

lemma `st_memory_grow`

```
∀ (v lc0 : List[Value]) (lim : Num) (R : GProp) (c : W32),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(c))]),
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim && R(gs, ms, h)),
    [Memory_grow],
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      st(
        APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(word(4294967295)))]),
        lc0,
        (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
          ms == lim && R(gs, lim, h),
        vs,
        lc,
        gs,
        ms,
        h)
      || DIV(lim, 65536) + val(c) <= 65536
         && st(
              APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(word(DIV(lim, 65536))))]),
              lc0,
              (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
                ms == lim + val(c) * 65536
                && star(
                     R(gs, lim),
                     pts(lim, REPLICATE(val(c) * 65536, word(0))),
                     h),
              vs,
              lc,
              gs,
              ms,
              h))
```

lemma `keep_lift_or`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (fr : HProp) (store : Wstore)
  (frame : Wframe) (vs : List[Value]) (P R : SAssn),
  keep(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    lift(
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        P(vs, lc, gs, ms, h) || R(vs, lc, gs, ms, h),
      fr),
    store,
    frame,
    vs) == (
    keep(fs, tabs, inst, lift(P, fr), store, frame, vs)
    || keep(fs, tabs, inst, lift(R, fr), store, frame, vs))
```

lemma `sl_or_g`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp P R Q : SAssn)
  (code : List[B_e]) (ls : List[SAssn])
if
  1. sl_triple_g(G, fs, tabs, inst, rp, P, code, Q, ls)
  2. sl_triple_g(G, fs, tabs, inst, rp, R, code, Q, ls)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      P(vs, lc, gs, ms, h) || R(vs, lc, gs, ms, h),
    code,
    Q,
    ls)
```

lemma `tlsf_physical_last`

```
∀ (d top : W32) (lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
then
  ∃ (ys : List[W32]), xs == APPEND(ys, [top])
```

lemma `tlsf_path_limit`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (a oldlim newlim b : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_path(ys, a, oldlim, hd, b)
  2. oldlim <= newlim
then
  tlsf_path(ys, a, newlim, hd, b)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_extend_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (p m : W32) (a oldlim newlim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, oldlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. oldlim <= newlim
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  4. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  5. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  6. val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == newlim
  7. tlsf_last(nh(m))
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, newlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_append_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (p m r : W32) (a oldlim newlim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, oldlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. oldlim <= newlim
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  4. tlsf_size(nh(m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  5. !tlsf_last(nh(m))
  6. tlsf_last(nh(r))
  7. val(r) == oldlim
  8. MOD(val(r), 8) == 0
  9. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(r)))
  10. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(r))), 8) == 0
  11. val(r) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(r))) == newlim
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, newlim, r, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_physical_extend_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m : W32) (oldlim newlim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, oldlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. oldlim <= newlim
  4. newlim <= 4294967296
  5. MOD(newlim, 65536) == 0
  6. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  7. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  8. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  9. val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == newlim
  10. tlsf_last(nh(m))
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, newlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_physical_append_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m r : W32) (oldlim newlim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, oldlim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(nh(m))
  3. oldlim <= newlim
  4. newlim <= 4294967296
  5. MOD(newlim, 65536) == 0
  6. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
  7. tlsf_size(nh(m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  8. !tlsf_last(nh(m))
  9. tlsf_last(nh(r))
  10. val(r) == oldlim
  11. MOD(val(r), 8) == 0
  12. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(r)))
  13. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(r))), 8) == 0
  14. val(r) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(nh(r))) == newlim
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, newlim, r, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_round_max`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(tlsf_round(s)) <= 4160749568
```

lemma `tlsf_grow_limits`

```
∀ (lim : Num) (pages : W32)
if
  1. MOD(lim, 65536) == 0
  2. DIV(lim, 65536) + val(pages) <= 65536
then
  lim <= lim + val(pages) * 65536
  && lim + val(pages) * 65536 <= 4294967296
  && MOD(lim + val(pages) * 65536, 65536) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_page_result_not_failure`

```
∀ (lim : Num)
if
  1. lim <= 4294967296
then
  !((word : (Num) -> W32)(DIV(lim, 65536)) == (word : (Num) -> W32)(4294967295))
```

lemma `tlsf_memory_byte_word`

```
∀ (lim : Num)
if
  1. MOD(lim, 65536) == 0
then
  (word_jshl : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(DIV(lim, 65536)),
    (word : (Num) -> W32)(16))
    == (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)
```

lemma `tlsf_limit_sub_value`

```
∀ (lim : Num) (m : W32)
if
  1. lim <= 4294967296
  2. 0 < val(m)
  3. val(m) <= lim
then
  val(word_sub(word(lim), m)) == lim - val(m)
```

lemma `tlsf_capacity_value`

```
∀ (lim : Num) (m : W32)
if
  1. lim <= 4294967296
  2. 0 < val(m)
  3. val(m) + 24 <= lim
then
  val(tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m)) == lim - val(m) - 8
```

lemma `tlsf_capacity_fields`

```
∀ (lim : Num) (m : W32)
if
  1. lim <= 4294967296
  2. 0 < val(m)
  3. val(m) + 24 <= lim
  4. MOD(lim, 8) == 0
  5. MOD(val(m), 8) == 0
then
  16 <= val(tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m))
  && MOD(val(tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m)), 8) == 0
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m)) == tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m)
  && val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_capacity_at(lim, m)) == lim
```

lemma `tlsf_page_aligned8`

```
∀ (lim : Num)
if
  1. MOD(lim, 65536) == 0
then
  MOD(lim, 8) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_top_facts`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
then
  lim <= 4294967296
  && MOD(lim, 65536) == 0
  && 0 < val(m)
  && MOD(val(m), 8) == 0
  && 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  && val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) == lim
  && tlsf_last(hd(m))
  && !MEM(m, ys)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_old_bound`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m x : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. MEM(x, APPEND(ys, [m]))
then
  val(x) < lim
```

lemma `tlsf_shr16_value`

```
∀ (x : W32), val(word_jushr(x, word(16))) == DIV(val(x), 65536)
```

lemma `tlsf_ceil_pages`

```
∀ (x : Num),
  x <= DIV(x + 65535, 65536) * 65536
  && DIV(x + 65535, 65536) * 65536 < x + 65536
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_expected_bounds`

```
∀ (s : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(s)
  2. val(s) <= 4160749568
then
  val(s) <= val(tlsf_ensure_expected(s))
  && 16 <= val(tlsf_ensure_expected(s))
  && val(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)) <= 4160749568
```

lemma `tlsf_used_pages_value`

```
∀ (e : W32)
if
  1. val(e) <= 4160749568
then
  val(word_jushr(word_add(e, word(65543)), word(16)))
    == DIV(val(e) + 65543, 65536)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pages_value`

```
∀ (e z : W32)
if
  1. val(e) <= 4160749568
  2. val(z) < val(e)
then
  val(word_jushr(word_add(word_sub(e, z), word(65535)), word(16)))
    == DIV(val(e) - val(z) + 65535, 65536)
```

lemma `tlsf_used_pages_fit`

```
∀ (e : W32)
if
  1. 16 <= val(e)
  2. val(e) <= 4160749568
then
  0 < val(word_jushr(word_add(e, word(65543)), word(16)))
  && val(e) + 8 <= val(word_jushr(word_add(e, word(65543)), word(16))) * 65536
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pages_fit`

```
∀ (e z : W32)
if
  1. val(e) <= 4160749568
then
  val(e) <= val(z) + val(
    word_jushr(word_add(word_sub(e, z), word(65535)), word(16))) * 65536
```

lemma `tlsf_resized_fields`

```
∀ (z h : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(z) == z
then
  tlsf_size(word_or(z, word_and(h, word(3)))) == z
  && tlsf_used(word_or(z, word_and(h, word(3)))) == tlsf_used(h)
  && tlsf_last(word_or(z, word_and(h, word(3)))) == tlsf_last(h)
```

lemma `tlsf_clear_last_fields`

```
∀ (h : W32),
  tlsf_size(word_and(h, word(4294967293))) == tlsf_size(h)
  && tlsf_used(word_and(h, word(4294967293))) == tlsf_used(h)
  && !tlsf_last(word_and(h, word(4294967293)))
```

lemma `tlsf_new_top_fields`

```
∀ (z : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_size(z) == z
then
  tlsf_size(word_or(z, word(2))) == z
  && !tlsf_used(word_or(z, word(2)))
  && tlsf_last(word_or(z, word(2)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_metadata_address`

```
∀ (d : W32)
if
  1. val(d) <= 4294965088
then
  val(tlsf_top_cell(d)) == val(tlsf_base(d)) + 1908
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_metadata_guards`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
then
  !word_ugt(d, word(4294965088))
  && !word_uge(word_jushr(tlsf_top_cell(d), word(16)), word(DIV(lim, 65536)))
```

lemma `tlsf_extend_model`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m : W32) (lim nextlim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. lim <= nextlim
  4. nextlim <= 4294967296
  5. MOD(nextlim, 65536) == 0
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(
    d,
    nextlim,
    m,
    APPEND(ys, [m]),
    tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, nextlim))
  && ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, nextlim, x)
  && tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, nextlim, m)
       == word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(nextlim, m), word_and(hd(m), word(3)))
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, nextlim, m))
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, nextlim, m))
       == tlsf_capacity_at(nextlim, m)
```

lemma `tlsf_append_model`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m : W32) (lim nextlim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. lim + 24 <= nextlim
  4. nextlim <= 4294967296
  5. MOD(nextlim, 65536) == 0
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(
    d,
    nextlim,
    word(lim),
    APPEND(ys, [m, word(lim)]),
    tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim))
  && ∀ x : W32,
       MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim, x)
  && tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim, m)
       == word_and(hd(m), word(4294967293))
  && tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim, word(lim))
       == word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(nextlim, word(lim)), word(2))
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim, word(lim)))
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, nextlim, word(lim)))
       == tlsf_capacity_at(nextlim, word(lim))
  && (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)) == lim
  && !MEM(word(lim), APPEND(ys, [m]))
```

lemma `tlsf_replicate_add`

```
∀ (n k : Num) (x : Byte),
  REPLICATE(n + k, x) == APPEND(REPLICATE(n, x), REPLICATE(k, x))
```

lemma `tlsf_zeros_bytes`

```
∀ (a n : Num) (h : Heap)
if
  1. pts(a, REPLICATE(n, word(0)), h)
then
  tlsf_bytes(a, n, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_le_zero_bytes`

```
∀ (n : Num), le_bytes(n, 0) == REPLICATE(n, word(0))
```

lemma `tlsf_zeros_word`

```
∀ (a n : Num)
if
  1. 4 <= n
then
  pts(a, REPLICATE(n, word(0))) == star(
    pts(a, serialise_i32(word(0))),
    pts(a + 4, REPLICATE(n - 4, word(0))))
```

lemma `tlsf_grown_bytes`

```
∀ (a n k : Num) (h : Heap)
if
  1. star(tlsf_bytes(a, n), pts(a + n, REPLICATE(k, word(0))), h)
then
  tlsf_bytes(a, n + k, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_bins_match_publish`

```
∀ (xs ys : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (r : W32) (f j : Num)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. !(MEM(r, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(r)))
  3. f < 28
  4. j < 16
  5. MEM(r, ys)
  6. !tlsf_used(nh(r))
  7. tlsf_level_of(nh(r)) == f
  8. tlsf_slot_of(nh(r)) == j
  9. ∀ m : W32,
       !(m == r) → (MEM(m, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(m)))
         == (MEM(m, ys) && !tlsf_used(nh(m)))
       && (
            MEM(m, xs)
            && !tlsf_used(hd(m)) → tlsf_level_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_level_of(nh(m))
            && tlsf_slot_of(hd(m)) == tlsf_slot_of(nh(m)))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(ys, nh, tlsf_bin_add(bl, f, j, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_extend_bins`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (m : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  2. MEM(m, xs)
  3. !tlsf_used(nh(m))
  4. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(nh(m)))
  5. ∀ x : W32, !(x == m) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_bins_match(xs, nh, tlsf_push_bl(nh(m), m, bl))
```

lemma `tlsf_append_bins`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (m r rh : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !MEM(r, xs)
  4. !tlsf_used(rh)
  5. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(rh))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(
    APPEND(xs, [r]),
    tlsf_put(tlsf_put(hd, m, word_and(hd(m), word(4294967293))), r, rh),
    tlsf_push_bl(rh, r, bl))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_extend_close`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m fl : W32) (lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), nh)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), nh, bl)
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  star(
    pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(m)),
    star(
      tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
      star(
        pts(val(m), serialise_i32(nh(m))),
        star(
          pts(
            val(word_add(m, word(4))),
            serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
          star(tlsf_spare(m, nh(m)), star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp))))))
    == star(tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), nh, fl, sb, bl), fp)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_append_close`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m fl r : W32) (lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, r, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), nh)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m, r]), nh, bl)
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, ys) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  star(
    pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(r)),
    star(
      tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
      star(
        pts(val(m), serialise_i32(nh(m))),
        star(
          pts(
            val(word_add(m, word(4))),
            serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
          star(
            tlsf_spare(m, nh(m)),
            star(
              pts(val(r), serialise_i32(nh(r))),
              star(
                pts(val(word_add(r, word(4))), serialise_i32(m)),
                star(
                  tlsf_spare(r, nh(r)),
                  star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp)))))))))
    == star(tlsf_large(d, lim, r, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), nh, fl, sb, bl), fp)
```

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_ensure_prefix(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_ensure_prefix_before_size(d, pop, push),
    CONS(Memory_size, tlsf_ensure_prefix_after_size(d, pop, push)))
```

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_ensure_used_write(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_ensure_used_before_size(d, pop, push),

```

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_ensure_free_write(d, pop, push) == APPEND(

    CONS(Memory_size, tlsf_ensure_free_after_size(d, pop, push)))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_used_body`

```
tlsf_ensure_used_body((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) == APPEND(
  tlsf_ensure_used_amount(d, pop, push),
  CONS(
    Memory_grow,
    APPEND(
      tlsf_ensure_guard(d, pop, push),
      APPEND(
        tlsf_ensure_used_write(d, pop, push),
        [Local_get(1), Call(push), Drop]))))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_free_body`

```
tlsf_ensure_free_body((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) == APPEND(
  tlsf_ensure_free_amount(d, pop, push),
  CONS(
    Memory_grow,
    APPEND(
      tlsf_ensure_guard(d, pop, push),
      APPEND(
        [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
        APPEND(
          tlsf_ensure_free_write(d, pop, push),
          [Local_get(0), Call(push), Drop])))))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_code_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_ensure_code(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_ensure_prefix(d, pop, push),
    [
      If(
        Tbv(NONE),
        tlsf_ensure_used_body(d, pop, push),
        tlsf_ensure_free_body(d, pop, push)),
      EConstNum(ConstInt32(word(1)))])
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_ro`

```
tlsf_ensure_ro(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hdr : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (before : Heap),
  (fp : GProp),
  (gs : List[Value]),
  (ms : Num),
  (h : Heap)) == (
  ms == lim
  && h == before
  && star(
       pts(
         val(
           word_add(
             word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
             word(1908))),
         serialise_i32(m)),
       star(pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hdr)), fp(gs, lim)),
       h))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_ro_top`

```
∀ (d m hdr : W32) (lim ms : Num) (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp)
  (gs : List[Value])
if
  1. tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp, gs, ms, h)
then
  has_i32(
    h,
    val(
      word_add(word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)), word(1908))),
    m)
```

lemma `st_ensure_load_top`

```
∀ (d m hdr : W32) (lim : Num) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp) (v lc : List[Value]),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(
        v,
        [
          V_num(
            ConstInt32(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(1908))))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)),
    [Load(T_i32, NONE, 2, 0)],
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]),
      lc,
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)))
```

```
∀ (d m hdr : W32) (lim ms : Num) (before h : Heap) (fp : GProp)

if

then
  has_i32(h, val(m), hdr)
```

lemma `st_ensure_load_header`

```
∀ (d m hdr : W32) (lim : Num) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp) (v lc : List[Value]),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      APPEND(v, [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]),
      lc,

```

example `tlsf_ensure_prefix_before_size_raw`

```
∀ (d m hdr s : W32) (lim pop push : Num) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !word_ugt(d, word(4294965088))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)),
    tlsf_ensure_prefix_before_size(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_jushr(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(1908)),
              word(16))))],
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_add(
              word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
              word(1908)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)))
```

example `tlsf_ensure_prefix_after_size_raw`

```
∀ (d m hdr s : W32) (lim pop push : Num) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !word_uge(
       word_jushr(
         word_add(
           word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
           word(1908)),
         word(16)),
       word(DIV(lim, 65536)))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_jushr(
              word_add(
                word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
                word(1908)),
              word(16)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(word(DIV(lim, 65536))))],
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_add(
              word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
              word(1908)))),
        V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)),
    tlsf_ensure_prefix_after_size(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word_and(hdr, word(1))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_prefix_raw`

```
∀ (d m hdr s : W32) (lim pop push : Num) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !word_ugt(d, word(4294965088))
  2. !word_uge(
       word_jushr(
         word_add(
           word_and(word_add(d, word(15)), word(4294967280)),
           word(1908)),
         word(16)),
       word(DIV(lim, 65536)))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)),
    tlsf_ensure_prefix(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word_and(hdr, word(1))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)))],
      tlsf_ensure_ro(d, m, hdr, lim, before, fp)))
```

example `tlsf_ensure_pop_invoke_g`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (us vs : List[W32])
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  2. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    tlsf_proved_spec(
      sfunc_ind(inst, pop),
      tlsf_pop_code(d),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      1,
      0),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(
                tlsf_bins(
                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
                fp(gs, ms)))))),
    NIL)
```

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)

  2. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  3. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(

    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(

    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],

      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(

                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),

                fp(gs, ms)))))),

```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_pop_detach`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m n top fl : W32)
  (pop lim extent : Num) (ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, bl)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  5. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == extent
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(n))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == extent
        && star(
             tlsf_detached(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               zs,
               hd,
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(m), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(m), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_detached_grow`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m fl : W32) (lim delta : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp) (heap0 : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. lim + delta <= 4294967296
  4. star(
       tlsf_detached(d, lim, m, ys, m, NIL, hd, fl, sb, bl),
       star(pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))), fp),
       heap0)
then
  star(
    tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, m))),
    star(
      pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(m)),
      star(
        tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
          star(
            pts(
              val(word_add(m, word(4))),
              serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
            star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp))))),
    heap0)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_free_tail_raw`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m hdr e fl pf0 nf0 : W32)
  (pop push lim2 : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. MOD(lim2, 65536) == 0
  3. !(m == word(0))
  4. 16 <= val(
       tlsf_size(word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hdr, word(3)))))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hdr)),
          (h : Heap) =>
            ms == lim2
            && (
                 (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
                   star(
                     pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
                     star(
                       pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
                       star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))))(
                 gs,
                 ms,
                 h))),
    APPEND(
      tlsf_ensure_free_write(d, pop, push),
      [Local_get(0), Call(push), Drop]),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(
            val(m),
            serialise_i32(
              word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hdr, word(3))))),
          star(
            tlsf_bins(
              d,
              tlsf_push_fl(
                word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hdr, word(3))),
                fl),
              tlsf_push_sb(
                word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hdr, word(3))),
                sb),
              tlsf_push_bl(
                word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, m), word_and(hdr, word(3))),
                m,
                bl)),
            (h : Heap) => ms == lim2 && fp(gs, ms, h)))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_growth_fit`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d s m : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. 16 <= val(s)
  3. val(s) <= 4160749568
  4. DIV(lim, 65536) + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) <= 65536
then
  lim <= lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536
  && lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536 <= 4294967296
  && MOD(lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536, 65536) == 0
  && tlsf_physical_ok(
       d,
       lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536,
       tlsf_grow_top(hd, m, lim),
       tlsf_grow_chain(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, m, lim),
       tlsf_grow_headers(
         hd,
         m,
         lim,
         lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536))
  && !tlsf_used(
       tlsf_grow_headers(
         hd,
         m,
         lim,
         lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536,
         tlsf_grow_top(hd, m, lim)))
  && val(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)) <= val(
       tlsf_size(
         tlsf_grow_headers(
           hd,
           m,
           lim,
           lim + val(tlsf_ensure_pages(s, hd(m))) * 65536,
           tlsf_grow_top(hd, m, lim))))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_grow_control`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp Q : SAssn)
  (d c : W32) (pop push lim : Num) (lc0 : List[Value]) (R : GProp)
  (rest : List[B_e]) (labels : List[SAssn])
if
  1. lim <= 4294967296
  2. ∀ gs : List[Value],
       ∀ ms : Num,
         ∀ h : Heap,
           (
             (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
               ms == lim && R(gs, lim, h))(gs, ms, h) → rp(
             [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
             lc0,
             gs,
             ms,
             h)
  3. DIV(lim, 65536) + val(c) <= 65536 → sl_triple_g(
       G,
       fs,
       tabs,
       inst,
       rp,
       st(
         NIL,
         lc0,
         (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
           ms == lim + val(c) * 65536
           && star(
                R(gs, lim),
                pts(lim, REPLICATE(val(c) * 65536, word(0))),
                h)),
       rest,
       Q,
       labels)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(c))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim && R(gs, ms, h)),
    APPEND([Memory_grow], APPEND(tlsf_ensure_guard(d, pop, push), rest)),
    Q,
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_append_grow`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d m fl : W32) (lim delta : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp) (heap0 : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. 24 <= delta
  4. lim + delta <= 4294967296
  5. star(
       tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, fl, sb, bl),
       star(pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))), fp),
       heap0)
then
  star(
    star(
      pts(
        (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)),
        serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
      star(
        pts(
          (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
              (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim),
              (word : (Num) -> W32)(4))),
          serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
        tlsf_bytes(
          (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)) + 8,
          (val : (W32) -> Num)(
            tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)))))),
    star(
      pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(m)),
      star(
        tlsf_regions((word : (Num) -> W32)(0), ys, hd),
        star(
          pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
          star(
            pts(
              (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                  m,
                  (word : (Num) -> W32)(4))),
              serialise_i32(tlsf_end((word : (Num) -> W32)(0), ys))),
            star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp))))),
    heap0)
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_frame`

```
tlsf_ensure_frame(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (ys : List[W32]),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
    star(
      tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
      star(
        pts(val(word_add(m, word(4))), serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
        star(
          tlsf_spare(m, hd(m)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, lim))))))
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_saved`

```
tlsf_ensure_saved(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (ys : List[W32]),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp),
  (before : Heap)) == (
  (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    ms == lim
    && h == before
    && star(
         tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, fl, sb, bl),
         fp(gs, lim),
         h))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_ro_saved`

```
∀ (d m fl : W32) (lim : Num) (ys : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
  (before : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
then
  tlsf_ensure_ro(
    d,
    m,
    hd(m),
    lim,
    before,
    tlsf_ensure_frame(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp))
    == tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before)
```

lemma `tlsf_star_forget_heap_eq`

```
∀ (p q : HProp) (before h : Heap)
if
  1. star((h0 : Heap) => h0 == before && p(h0), q, h)
then
  star(p, q, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_prefix_saved`

```
∀ (d m fl s : W32) (lim pop push : Num) (ys : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
  (before : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before)),
    tlsf_ensure_prefix(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word_and(hd(m), word(1))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(tlsf_ensure_expected(s)))],
      tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_free_amount_saved`

```
∀ (d m fl e : W32) (lim pop push : Num) (ys : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
  (before : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  2. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before)),
    tlsf_ensure_free_amount(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            word_jushr(
              word_add(word_sub(e, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(65535)),
              word(16))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_saved_growth_frame`

```
∀ (d m fl : W32) (lim delta : Num) (ys : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
  (before heap0 : Heap) (gs : List[Value])
if
  1. star(
       tlsf_ensure_saved(d, lim, ys, m, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, before, gs, lim),
       pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))),
       heap0)
then
  star(
    tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, fl, sb, bl),
    star(pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))), fp(gs, lim)),
    heap0)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_append`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m e fl : W32)
  (pop push lim lim2 : Num) (ys : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
  3. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
  5. lim + 24 <= lim2
  6. lim2 <= 4294967296
  7. MOD(lim2, 65536) == 0
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim2
        && star(
             tlsf_regions((word : (Num) -> W32)(0), ys, hd),
             star(
               pts(
                 (val : (W32) -> Num)(

                 serialise_i32(tlsf_end((word : (Num) -> W32)(0), ys))),

                 pts(

                   serialise_i32(m)),
                 star(
                   pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
                   star(
                     pts(
                       (val : (W32) -> Num)((word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)),
                       serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),

                       pts(
                         (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                           (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim),
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(4))),
                         serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
                       star(
                         tlsf_bytes(
                           (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                             (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)) + 8,
                           (val : (W32) -> Num)(
                             tlsf_capacity_at(
                               lim2,
                               (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)))),
                         star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))))))),
             h)),
    (APPEND : (List[B_e], List[B_e]) -> List[B_e])(
      tlsf_ensure_used_write(d, pop, push),
      [Local_get(1), Call(push), Drop]),
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32((word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim2
        && star(
             tlsf_large(
               d,
               lim2,
               (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim),
               (APPEND : (List[W32], List[W32]) -> List[W32])(
                 ys,
                 [m, (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)]),
               tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, lim2),
               tlsf_push_fl(
                 (word_or : (W32, W32) -> W32)(
                   tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)),
                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(2)),

                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(2)),
                 sb),
               tlsf_push_bl(

                   tlsf_capacity_at(lim2, (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim)),
                   (word : (Num) -> W32)(2)),
                 (word : (Num) -> W32)(lim),

```

```
       inst,

       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
     && tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
     && tlsf_used(hd(m))
     && tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
     && lim + 24 <= lim + delta
     && lim + delta <= 4294967296
     && MOD(lim + delta, 65536) == 0
  2. 24 <= delta
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim + delta
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             star(pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))), fp(gs, ms)),
             h)),
    APPEND(
      tlsf_ensure_used_write(d, pop, push),
      [Local_get(1), Call(push), Drop]),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(lim)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim + delta
        && star(
             tlsf_large(
               d,
               lim + delta,
               word(lim),
               APPEND(ys, [m, word(lim)]),
               tlsf_append_headers(hd, m, lim, lim + delta),
               tlsf_push_fl(
                 word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, word(lim)), word(2)),
                 fl),
               tlsf_push_sb(
                 word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, word(lim)), word(2)),
                 sb),
               tlsf_push_bl(
                 word_or(tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, word(lim)), word(2)),

             fp(gs, ms),

```

```
  6. star(

then

    hd,
    fl,
    sb,
    bl,
    before,
    fp,

    gs,
    ms,
    h)
```

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m e fl : W32)
  (pop push lim delta : Num) (ys us bs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)

       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
     && tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd)
     && tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, bl)
     && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
     && bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, bs))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  3. lim + delta <= 4294967296
  4. MOD(lim + delta, 65536) == 0
then

    inst,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim + delta
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             (
               (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
                 star(pts(lim, REPLICATE(delta, word(0))), fp(gs, ms)))(
               gs,
               ms),
             h)),
    APPEND(
      [Local_get(0), Call(pop)],
      APPEND(
        tlsf_ensure_free_write(d, pop, push),
        [Local_get(0), Call(push), Drop])),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(e))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim + delta
        && star(
             tlsf_large(
               d,
               lim + delta,
               m,
               APPEND(ys, [m]),
               tlsf_extend_headers(hd, m, lim + delta),
               tlsf_push_fl(
                 word_or(
                   tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, m),
                   word_and(hd(m), word(3))),
                 tlsf_pop_fl(hd(m), fl, sb, APPEND(us, bs))),
               tlsf_push_sb(
                 word_or(
                   tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, m),
                   word_and(hd(m), word(3))),
                 tlsf_pop_sb(hd(m), sb, APPEND(us, bs))),
               tlsf_push_bl(
                 word_or(
                   tlsf_capacity_at(lim + delta, m),
                   word_and(hd(m), word(3))),
                 m,
                 tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, bs)))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

```
  7. !tlsf_used(hd(m))

```

```
  5. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  6. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  7. tlsf_tables_sync(fl, sb, bl)
then
  sl_triple_g(
```
