      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(s)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && h == before
        && star(tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, lim), h)),
    tlsf_ensure_code(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_ensure_post(
        d,
        s,
        lim,
        top,
        xs,
        hd,
        fl,
        sb,
        bl,
        before,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    [
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        tlsf_ensure_post(
          d,
          s,
          lim,
          top,
          xs,
          hd,
          fl,
          sb,
          bl,
          before,
          fp,
          vs,
          gs,
          ms,
          h)])
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_body`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d s top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp)
  (args : List[Value])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_ensure_post(
        d,
        s,
        lim,
        top,
        xs,
        hd,
        fl,
        sb,
        bl,
        before,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      vs == NIL
      && lc == APPEND(args, [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))])
      && tlsf_ensure_pre(
           d,
           s,
           lim,
           top,
           xs,
           hd,
           fl,
           sb,
           bl,
           before,
           fp,
           args,
           gs,
           ms,
           h),
    tlsf_ensure_code(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_ensure_post(
        d,
        s,
        lim,
        top,
        xs,
        hd,
        fl,
        sb,
        bl,
        before,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    [
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        tlsf_ensure_post(
          d,
          s,
          lim,
          top,
          xs,
          hd,
          fl,
          sb,
          bl,
          before,
          fp,
          vs,
          gs,
          ms,
          h)])
```

definition `tlsf_ensure_spec`

```
tlsf_ensure_spec(
  (inst : Inst),
  (d : W32),
  (pop : Num),
  (push : Num),
  (ensure_idx : Num),
  (a : Num),
  (P : FProp),
  (Q : FProp)) == (
  a == sfunc_ind(inst, ensure_idx)
  && (
       ∃ s : W32,
         ∃ lim : Num,
           ∃ top : W32,
             ∃ xs : List[W32],
               ∃ hd : (W32) -> W32,
                 ∃ fl : W32,
                   ∃ sb : (Num) -> W32,
                     ∃ bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                       ∃ before : Heap,
                         ∃ fp : GProp,
                           P == tlsf_ensure_pre(
                             d,
                             s,
                             lim,
                             top,
                             xs,
                             hd,
                             fl,
                             sb,
                             bl,
                             before,
                             fp)
                           && Q == tlsf_ensure_post(
                                d,
                                s,
                                lim,
                                top,
                                xs,
                                hd,
                                fl,
                                sb,
                                bl,
                                before,
                                fp)))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_spec_call`

```
∀ (inst : Inst) (d s top fl : W32) (pop push ensure_idx lim : Num)
  (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap) (fp : GProp),
  tlsf_ensure_spec(
    inst,
    d,
    pop,
    push,
    ensure_idx,
    sfunc_ind(inst, ensure_idx),
    tlsf_ensure_pre(d, s, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, before, fp),
    tlsf_ensure_post(d, s, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, before, fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_specs`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d : W32)
  (pop push ensure_idx k : Num)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, ensure_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_ensure_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  specs_ok(k, tlsf_ensure_spec(inst, d, pop, push, ensure_idx), fs, tabs, inst)
```

lemma `tlsf_ensure_all`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d s top fl : W32)
  (pop push ensure_idx lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (before : Heap)
  (fp : GProp) (vs0 lc0 : List[Value])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, ensure_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_ensure_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ args : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, args)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_ensure_pre(
             d,
             s,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             before,
             fp,
             args,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    [Call(ensure_idx)],
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ res : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, res)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_ensure_post(
             d,
             s,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             before,
             fp,
             res,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    NIL)
```

program `tlsf_free_next`

```
tlsf_free_next((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) ==
  local.get 0
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 2
  i32.and
  if (result i32)
    i32.const 1
  else
    local.get 0
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 4294967292
    i32.and
    i32.add
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 1
    i32.and
  end
  i32.eqz
  if
    local.get 0
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 4294967292
    i32.and
    i32.add
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    local.tee 1
    call pop
    local.get 0
    local.get 0
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    local.tee 2
    i32.const 3
    i32.and
    local.get 2
    i32.const 4294967292
    i32.and
    local.get 1
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 4294967292
    i32.and
    i32.add
    i32.const 8
    i32.add
    i32.or
    i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    local.get 1
    i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
    i32.const 2
    i32.and
    if
      local.get 0
      local.get 0
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 4294967293
      i32.and
      i32.const 2
      i32.or
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const d
      i32.const 15
      i32.add
      i32.const 4294967280
      i32.and
      i32.const 1908
      i32.add
      local.get 0
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    else
      local.get 1
      local.get 1
      i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
      i32.const 4294967292
      i32.and
      i32.add
      i32.const 12
      i32.add
      local.get 0
      i32.store offset=0 align=2**2
    end
  end
```

program `tlsf_free_prev_args`

```
tlsf_free_prev_args((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) ==
  local.get 0
  i32.const 4
  i32.add
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  local.tee 1
```

program `tlsf_free_next_args`

```
tlsf_free_next_args((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) ==
  local.get 0
  local.get 0
  i32.load offset=0 align=2**2
  i32.const 4294967292
  i32.and
  i32.add
  i32.const 8
  i32.add
  local.tee 1
```

program `tlsf_free_publish`

```
tlsf_free_publish((d : W32), (pop : Num), (push : Num)) ==
  local.get 0
  call push
```

lemma `tlsf_free_prev_body_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_free_prev_body(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_free_prev_args(d, pop, push),
    APPEND([Call(pop)], tlsf_free_prev_write(d, pop, push)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_next_body_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_free_next_body(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_free_next_args(d, pop, push),
    APPEND([Call(pop)], tlsf_free_next_write(d, pop, push)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_prev_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_free_prev(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_free_prev_test(d, pop, push),
    [If(Tbv(NONE), tlsf_free_prev_body(d, pop, push), NIL)])
```

lemma `tlsf_free_next_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_free_next(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_free_next_test(d, pop, push),
    [If(Tbv(NONE), tlsf_free_next_body(d, pop, push), NIL)])
```

lemma `tlsf_free_code_parts`

```
∀ (d : W32) (pop push : Num),
  tlsf_free_code(d, pop, push) == APPEND(
    tlsf_free_clear(d, pop, push),
    APPEND(
      tlsf_free_prev(d, pop, push),
      APPEND(tlsf_free_next(d, pop, push), tlsf_free_publish(d, pop, push))))
```

definition `tlsf_release`

```
tlsf_release((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32))
  == tlsf_put(hd, m, word_and(hd(m), word(4294967294)))
```

definition `tlsf_merge_size`

```
tlsf_merge_size((a : W32), (b : W32))
  == word_add(word_add(tlsf_size(a), tlsf_size(b)), word(8))
```

definition `tlsf_merge_header`

```
tlsf_merge_header((a : W32), (b : W32))
  == word_or(tlsf_merge_size(a, b), word_and(b, word(2)))
```

definition `tlsf_merge_headers`

```
tlsf_merge_headers((hd : (W32) -> W32), (m : W32), (r : W32))
  == tlsf_put(hd, m, tlsf_merge_header(hd(m), hd(r)))
```

definition `tlsf_free_geom`

```
tlsf_free_geom(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (m : W32))
  == tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m))
```

definition `tlsf_free_pending`

```
tlsf_free_pending(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (ys : List[W32]),
  (m : W32),
  (zs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == (
  (h : Heap) =>
    tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
    && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
    && star(
         pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(top)),
         star(
           tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
           star(
             pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
             star(
               pts(
                 val(word_add(m, word(4))),
                 serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
               star(
                 tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
                 star(tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd), tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl)))))),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_free_pre`

```
tlsf_free_pre(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (args : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    args == [V_num(ConstInt32(m))]
    && ms == lim
    && MEM(m, xs)
    && tlsf_used(hd(m))
    && star(
         tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
         star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp(gs, ms)),
         h))
```

definition `tlsf_free_post`

```
tlsf_free_post(
  (d : W32),
  (m : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (top : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]),
  (fp : GProp)) == (
  (res : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    res == [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))]
    && ms == lim
    && (
         ∃ top2 : W32,
           ∃ xs2 : List[W32],
             ∃ hd2 : (W32) -> W32,
               ∃ fl2 : W32,
                 ∃ sb2 : (Num) -> W32,
                   ∃ bl2 : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                     ∃ r : W32,
                       MEM(r, xs2)
                       && !tlsf_used(hd2(r))
                       && val(r) <= val(m)
                       && val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) <= val(r) + 8 + val(
                            tlsf_size(hd2(r)))
                       && (
                            ∀ x : W32,
                              MEM(x, xs)
                              && !(x == m)
                              && tlsf_used(hd(x)) → MEM(x, xs2)
                              && hd2(x) == hd(x))
                       && star(
                            tlsf_large(d, lim, top2, xs2, hd2, fl2, sb2, bl2),
                            fp(gs, ms),
                            h)))
```

definition `tlsf_free_pair`

```
tlsf_free_pair(
  (d : W32),
  (top : W32),
  (ys : List[W32]),
  (m : W32),
  (r : W32),
  (zs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  (fl : W32),
  (sb : (Num) -> W32),
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])) == star(
  pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(top)),
  star(
    tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
    star(
      pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd(m))),
      star(
        pts(val(word_add(m, word(4))), serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
        star(
          tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
          star(
            pts(val(r), serialise_i32(hd(r))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(r, word(4))), serialise_i32(m)),
              star(
                tlsf_bytes(val(r) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))),
                star(tlsf_regions(r, zs, hd), tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl))))))))))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_end_append`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (p : W32),
  tlsf_end(p, APPEND(ys, zs)) == tlsf_end(tlsf_end(p, ys), zs)
```

definition `tlsf_free_left`

```
tlsf_free_left((ys : List[W32]), (hd : (W32) -> W32))
  == (ys == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))))
```

definition `tlsf_free_right`

```
tlsf_free_right((zs : List[W32]), (hd : (W32) -> W32))
  == (zs == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(HD(zs))))
```

definition `tlsf_free_covers`

```
tlsf_free_covers(
  (m0 : W32),
  (hd0 : (W32) -> W32),
  (m : W32),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
  val(m) <= val(m0)
  && val(m0) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd0(m0))) <= val(m) + 8 + val(
       tlsf_size(hd(m))))
```

definition `tlsf_free_live`

```
tlsf_free_live(
  (xs0 : List[W32]),
  (hd0 : (W32) -> W32),
  (m0 : W32),
  (xs : List[W32]),
  (hd : (W32) -> W32)) == (
  ∀ x : W32,
    MEM(x, xs0)
    && !(x == m0)
    && tlsf_used(hd0(x)) → MEM(x, xs)
    && hd(x) == hd0(x))
```

definition `tlsf_free_work`

```
tlsf_free_work(
  (d : W32),
  (lim : Num),
  (m0 : W32),
  (xs0 : List[W32]),
  (hd0 : (W32) -> W32),
  (fp : GProp),
  (left : Bool),
  (right : Bool)) == (
  (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
  (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
    vs == NIL
    && ms == lim
    && (
         ∃ top : W32,
           ∃ ys : List[W32],
             ∃ m : W32,
               ∃ zs : List[W32],
                 ∃ hd : (W32) -> W32,
                   ∃ fl : W32,
                     ∃ sb : (Num) -> W32,
                       ∃ bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                         ∃ l1 : Value,
                           ∃ l2 : Value,
                             lc == [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2]
                             && tlsf_free_geom(
                                  d,
                                  lim,
                                  top,
                                  APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
                                  hd,
                                  m)
                             && !tlsf_used(hd(m))
                             && tlsf_bins_match(
                                  APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
                                  tlsf_mark(hd, m),
                                  bl)
                             && tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, hd)
                             && tlsf_free_live(
                                  xs0,
                                  hd0,
                                  m0,
                                  APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
                                  hd)
                             && (!left || tlsf_free_left(ys, hd))
                             && (!right || tlsf_free_right(zs, hd))
                             && star(
                                  tlsf_free_pending(
                                    d,
                                    lim,
                                    top,
                                    ys,
                                    m,
                                    zs,
                                    hd,
                                    fl,
                                    sb,
                                    bl),
                                  fp(gs, ms),
                                  h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_release_fields`

```
∀ (h : W32),
  tlsf_size(word_and(h, word(4294967294))) == tlsf_size(h)
  && tlsf_last(word_and(h, word(4294967294))) == tlsf_last(h)
  && !tlsf_used(word_and(h, word(4294967294)))
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_fields`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. ∀ x : W32,
       MEM(x, xs) → tlsf_size(hd(x)) == tlsf_size(nh(x))
       && tlsf_last(hd(x)) == tlsf_last(nh(x))
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd) == tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_bins_transport`

```
∀ (xs ys : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  2. ∀ x : W32,
       (MEM(x, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(x))) == (MEM(x, ys) && !tlsf_used(nh(x)))
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, xs) && !tlsf_used(hd(x)) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_bins_match(ys, nh, bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_mark_fields`

```
∀ (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m x : W32),
  tlsf_size(tlsf_mark(hd, m, x)) == tlsf_size(hd(x))
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_mark(hd, m, x)) == tlsf_last(hd(x))
  && tlsf_used(tlsf_mark(hd, m, x)) == (x == m || tlsf_used(hd(x)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_release_fields`

```
∀ (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m x : W32),
  tlsf_size(tlsf_release(hd, m, x)) == tlsf_size(hd(x))
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_release(hd, m, x)) == tlsf_last(hd(x))
  && tlsf_used(tlsf_release(hd, m, x)) == (!(x == m) && tlsf_used(hd(x)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_layout_mark`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p top m : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m))
    == tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_geom_facts`

```
∀ (d top m : W32) (lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, xs, hd, m) == (
    val(d) <= 4294965088
    && lim <= 4294967296
    && MOD(lim, 65536) == 0
    && !(xs == NIL)
    && tlsf_layout(word(0), tlsf_origin(d), lim, top, xs, hd)
    && tlsf_coalesced(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, m)))
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_prev`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p q top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. !(xs == NIL)
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, xs, hd) == tlsf_layout(q, a, lim, top, xs, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_contract_head`

```
∀ (zs : List[W32]) (p top m r : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs)), hd)
  2. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))
  3. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  4. tlsf_last(nh(m)) == tlsf_last(hd(r))
  5. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, zs) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, if zs == NIL { m } else { top }, CONS(m, zs), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_layout_contract`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (p top m r : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  2. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))
  3. MOD(val(tlsf_size(nh(m))), 8) == 0
  4. tlsf_last(nh(m)) == tlsf_last(hd(r))
  5. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, APPEND(ys, zs)) → hd(x) == nh(x)
then
  tlsf_layout(
    p,
    a,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { m } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_coalesced_focus`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32),
  tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd) == (
    tlsf_coalesced(ys, hd)
    && tlsf_coalesced(zs, hd)
    && (ys == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))) || tlsf_used(hd(m)))
    && (zs == NIL || tlsf_used(hd(m)) || tlsf_used(hd(HD(zs)))))
```

lemma `tlsf_coalesced_contract`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r : W32) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(nh(m))
  3. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, APPEND(ys, zs)) → tlsf_used(hd(x)) == tlsf_used(nh(x))
then
  tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_end_member`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (p : W32)
if
  1. !(xs == NIL)
then
  MEM(tlsf_end(p, xs), xs)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_ready_cong`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, xs) → tlsf_used(hd(x)) == tlsf_used(nh(x))
then
  tlsf_free_left(xs, hd) == tlsf_free_left(xs, nh)
  && tlsf_free_right(xs, hd) == tlsf_free_right(xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_left_transfer`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_left(xs, hd)
  2. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, xs) → tlsf_used(hd(x)) == tlsf_used(nh(x))
then
  tlsf_free_left(xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_right_transfer`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_right(xs, hd)
  2. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, xs) → tlsf_used(hd(x)) == tlsf_used(nh(x))
then
  tlsf_free_right(xs, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_coalesced_neighbours`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_coalesced(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
then
  tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
  && tlsf_free_right(zs, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_ready_physical`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  2. tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
  3. tlsf_free_right(zs, hd)
then
  tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_merge_aligned`

```
∀ (a b : Num)
if
  1. MOD(a, 8) == 0
  2. MOD(b, 8) == 0
then
  MOD(a + b + 8, 8) == 0
```

lemma `tlsf_merge_size_fields`

```
∀ (a b : W32)
if
  1. val(tlsf_size(a)) + val(tlsf_size(b)) + 8 < 4294967296
  2. MOD(val(tlsf_size(a)), 8) == 0
  3. MOD(val(tlsf_size(b)), 8) == 0
then
  val(tlsf_merge_size(a, b)) == val(tlsf_size(a)) + val(tlsf_size(b)) + 8
  && MOD(val(tlsf_merge_size(a, b)), 8) == 0
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_size(a, b)) == tlsf_merge_size(a, b)
```

lemma `tlsf_merge_header_fields`

```
∀ (a b : W32)
if
  1. val(tlsf_size(a)) + val(tlsf_size(b)) + 8 < 4294967296
  2. MOD(val(tlsf_size(a)), 8) == 0
  3. MOD(val(tlsf_size(b)), 8) == 0
then
  tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_header(a, b)) == tlsf_merge_size(a, b)
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_merge_header(a, b))
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_merge_header(a, b)) == tlsf_last(b)
  && val(tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_header(a, b)))
       == val(tlsf_size(a)) + val(tlsf_size(b)) + 8
```

lemma `tlsf_free_flags_split`

```
∀ (h : W32),
  word_and(h, word(3)) == word_or(word_and(h, word(1)), word_and(h, word(2)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_low_flags`

```
∀ (h : W32)
if
  1. !tlsf_used(h)
  2. !tlsf_last(h)
then
  word_and(h, word(3)) == word(0)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_store_last`

```
∀ (hm hr : W32)
if
  1. !tlsf_used(hm)
  2. !tlsf_last(hm)
  3. tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr)) == tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr)
  4. tlsf_last(hr)
then
  word_or(
    word_and(
      word_or(word_and(hm, word(3)), tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr)),
      word(4294967293)),
    word(2))
    == tlsf_merge_header(hm, hr)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_store_next`

```
∀ (hm hr : W32)
if
  1. !tlsf_used(hm)
  2. !tlsf_last(hm)
  3. tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr)) == tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr)
  4. !tlsf_last(hr)
then
  word_or(word_and(hm, word(3)), tlsf_merge_size(hm, hr))
    == tlsf_merge_header(hm, hr)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_begin_model`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (m d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
then
  tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, xs, tlsf_release(hd, m), m)
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_release(hd, m, m))
  && tlsf_size(tlsf_release(hd, m, m)) == tlsf_size(hd(m))
  && tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(tlsf_release(hd, m), m), bl)
  && ∀ x : W32, !(x == m) → tlsf_release(hd, m, x) == hd(x)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_release_live`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m : W32),
  tlsf_free_live(xs, hd, m, xs, tlsf_release(hd, m))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_release_covers`

```
∀ (hd : (W32) -> W32) (m : W32), tlsf_free_covers(m, hd, m, tlsf_release(hd, m))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_live`

```
∀ (xs0 ys zs : List[W32]) (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (m0 m r : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
then
  tlsf_free_live(
    xs0,
    hd0,
    m0,
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_covers`

```
∀ (m0 active m r : W32) (hd0 hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, active, hd)
  2. active == m || active == r
  3. val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  4. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))
then
  tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, nh)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_work_fold`

```
∀ (d m0 top m fl : W32) (lim ms : Num) (xs0 ys zs : List[W32])
  (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (fp : GProp) (left right : Bool) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 : Value) (vs lc gs : List[Value])
  (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  4. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, hd)
  5. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  6. !left || tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
  7. !right || tlsf_free_right(zs, hd)
  8. st(
       NIL,
       [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
       (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
         ms == lim
         && star(
              tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, ys, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
              fp(gs, ms),
              h),
       vs,
       lc,
       gs,
       ms,
       h)
then
  tlsf_free_work(d, lim, m0, xs0, hd0, fp, left, right, vs, lc, gs, ms, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pair_facts`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r p top : W32) (a lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_layout(p, a, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  2. lim <= 4294967296
then
  tlsf_block_ok(val(m), lim, m, hd)
  && tlsf_block_ok(val(r), lim, r, hd)
  && val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  && !tlsf_last(hd(m))
  && tlsf_last(hd(r)) == (zs == NIL)
  && !MEM(m, ys)
  && !MEM(m, zs)
  && !MEM(r, ys)
  && !MEM(r, zs)
  && !(m == r)
  && val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + val(tlsf_size(hd(r))) + 8 < 4294967296
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pair_geometry`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r d top active : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, active)
then
  tlsf_block_ok(val(m), lim, m, hd)
  && tlsf_block_ok(val(r), lim, r, hd)
  && val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  && !tlsf_last(hd(m))
  && tlsf_last(hd(r)) == (zs == NIL)
  && !MEM(m, ys)
  && !MEM(m, zs)
  && !MEM(r, ys)
  && !MEM(r, zs)
  && !(m == r)
  && val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + val(tlsf_size(hd(r))) + 8 < 4294967296
```

lemma `tlsf_free_successor`

```
∀ (m r h : W32)
if
  1. val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(h))
then
  r == word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(h)), word(8))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_successor_prev`

```
∀ (m r h : W32)
if
  1. r == word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(h)), word(8))
then
  word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(h)), word(12)) == word_add(r, word(4))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_member`

```
∀ (d top active x : W32) (lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, xs, hd, active)
  2. MEM(x, xs)
then
  tlsf_block_ok(val(x), lim, x, hd)
  && lim <= 4294967296
```

lemma `tlsf_free_last`

```
∀ (ys : List[W32]) (d top m : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, [m]), hd, m)
then
  top == m
  && tlsf_last(hd(m))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_next_geometry`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (d top m n : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(n, zs))), hd, m)
then
  !tlsf_last(hd(m))
  && n == word_add(word_add(m, tlsf_size(hd(m))), word(8))
  && !(m == n)
  && tlsf_block_ok(val(n), lim, n, hd)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_bins`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r a b : W32) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))),
       tlsf_mark(tlsf_mark(hd, a), b),
       bl)
  2. a == m && b == r || a == r && b == m
  3. !(m == r)
  4. !MEM(r, ys)
  5. !MEM(r, zs)
then
  tlsf_bins_match(
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    tlsf_mark(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r), m),
    bl)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_bin_decompose`

```
∀ (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (a b : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, a), bl)
  2. !(a == b)
  3. MEM(b, xs)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(b))
  5. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd(b)))
then
  ∃ (us vs : List[W32]),
    bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(b)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(b))) == APPEND(us, CONS(b, vs))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pop_bins_pair`

```
∀ (xs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (a b : W32)
if
  1. tlsf_bins_match(xs, tlsf_mark(hd, a), bl)
  2. !(a == b)
  3. MEM(b, xs)
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(b))
  5. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(b)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(b))) == APPEND(us, CONS(b, vs))
then
  tlsf_bins_match(
    xs,
    tlsf_mark(tlsf_mark(hd, a), b),
    tlsf_pop_bl(hd(b), bl, APPEND(us, vs)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_model`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r active d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, active)
  2. active == m || active == r
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
then
  tlsf_free_geom(
    d,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { m } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
    m)
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, m))
  && val(tlsf_size(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, m)))
       == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))
  && tlsf_last(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, m)) == tlsf_last(hd(r))
  && ∀ x : W32,
       MEM(x, APPEND(ys, zs)) → hd(x) == tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, x)
  && !(m == r)
  && !MEM(m, ys)
  && !MEM(m, zs)
  && !MEM(r, ys)
  && !MEM(r, zs)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_prev_ready`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, r)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
then
  tlsf_free_left(ys, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_next_ready`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m r d top : W32) (lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, m)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  4. tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
then
  tlsf_free_left(ys, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_right(zs, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_prev_work`

```
∀ (xs0 ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (m0 m r d top : W32)
  (lim : Num) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, r)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), tlsf_mark(hd, r), bl)
  5. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, r, hd)
  6. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  7. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  tlsf_free_geom(
    d,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { m } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
    m)
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, m))
  && tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
       tlsf_mark(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r), m),
       tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs)))
  && tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_live(
       xs0,
       hd0,
       m0,
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
       tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_left(ys, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_next_work`

```
∀ (xs0 ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (m0 m r d top : W32)
  (lim : Num) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, m)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  5. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, hd)
  6. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd)
  7. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(r)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(r))) == APPEND(us, CONS(r, vs))
  8. tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
then
  tlsf_free_geom(
    d,
    lim,
    if zs == NIL { m } else { top },
    APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
    tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
    m)
  && !tlsf_used(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r, m))
  && tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
       tlsf_mark(tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r), m),
       tlsf_pop_bl(hd(r), bl, APPEND(us, vs)))
  && tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_live(
       xs0,
       hd0,
       m0,
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
       tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_left(ys, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
  && tlsf_free_right(zs, tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r))
```

lemma `tlsf_bytes_two_words`

```
∀ (a : Num),
  tlsf_bytes(a, 8) == (
    (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ x : W32,
        ∃ y : W32,
          star(pts(a, serialise_i32(x)), pts(a + 4, serialise_i32(y)), h))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_join_bytes`

```
∀ (a n b : Num) (x y : W32) (R : HProp) (h : Heap)
if
  1. star(
       tlsf_bytes(a, n),
       star(
         pts(a + n, serialise_i32(x)),
         star(
           pts(a + n + 4, serialise_i32(y)),
           star(tlsf_bytes(a + n + 8, b), R))),
       h)
then
  star(tlsf_bytes(a, n + 8 + b), R, h)
```

example `tlsf_free_clear_raw`

```
∀ (d m h : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts(val(m), serialise_i32(h)), fp(gs, ms))),
    tlsf_free_clear(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(word_and(h, word(4294967294)))),
          fp(gs, ms))))
```

example `tlsf_free_prev_zero_raw`

```
∀ (d m h p : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. true
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(
            (val : (W32) -> Num)(
              (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(m, (word : (Num) -> W32)(4))),
            serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
          fp(gs, ms))),
    tlsf_free_prev_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            wasm_bool((word : (Num) -> W32)(1) == (word : (Num) -> W32)(0))))],
      [
        V_num(ConstInt32(m)),
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(m, (word : (Num) -> W32)(4)))),
        l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(
            (val : (W32) -> Num)(
              (word_add : (W32, W32) -> W32)(m, (word : (Num) -> W32)(4))),
            serialise_i32((word : (Num) -> W32)(0))),
          fp(gs, ms))))
```

example `tlsf_free_prev_nonzero_raw`

```
∀ (d m h p : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(p == word(0))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(word_add(m, word(4))), serialise_i32(p)),
          star(pts(val(p), serialise_i32(h)), fp(gs, ms)))),
    tlsf_free_prev_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(wasm_bool(word_and(h, word(1)) == word(0))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word_add(m, word(4)))), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(word_add(m, word(4))), serialise_i32(p)),
          star(pts(val(p), serialise_i32(h)), fp(gs, ms)))))
```

example `tlsf_free_next_last_raw`

```
∀ (d m h nh : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(word_and(h, word(2)) == word(0))
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      (NIL : List[Value]),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(h)), fp(gs, ms))),
    tlsf_free_next_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [
        V_num(
          ConstInt32(
            wasm_bool((word : (Num) -> W32)(1) == (word : (Num) -> W32)(0))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts((val : (W32) -> Num)(m), serialise_i32(h)), fp(gs, ms))))
```

example `tlsf_free_next_nonlast_raw`

```
∀ (d m h nh : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. word_and(h, word(2)) == word(0)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(h)),
          star(
            pts(
              val(
                word_add(
                  word_add(m, word_and(h, word(4294967292))),
                  word(8))),
              serialise_i32(nh)),
            fp(gs, ms)))),
    tlsf_free_next_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(wasm_bool(word_and(nh, word(1)) == word(0))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(h)),
          star(
            pts(
              val(
                word_add(
                  word_add(m, word_and(h, word(4294967292))),
                  word(8))),
              serialise_i32(nh)),
            fp(gs, ms)))))
```

example `tlsf_free_prev_args_raw`

```
∀ (d m r hm : W32) (pop push : Num) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp),
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts(val(word_add(r, word(4))), serialise_i32(m)), fp(gs, ms))),
    tlsf_free_prev_args(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(pts(val(word_add(r, word(4))), serialise_i32(m)), fp(gs, ms))))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_if_taken`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp Q : SAssn)
  (lc : List[Value]) (R : GProp) (c : W32) (body : List[B_e])
  (labels : List[SAssn])
if
  1. !(c == word(0))
  2. ∀ ls2 : List[SAssn],
       sl_triple_g(G, fs, tabs, inst, rp, st(NIL, lc, R), body, Q, ls2)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st([V_num(ConstInt32(c))], lc, R),
    [If(Tbv(NONE), body, NIL)],
    Q,
    labels)
```

  (labels : List[SAssn])
if

  2. ∀ ls2 : List[SAssn],

then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st([V_num(ConstInt32(c))], lc, R),

    Q,
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_prev_boundary_test`

```
∀ (ys qs zs ts : List[W32]) (m p n d top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. true
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(NIL, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(NIL, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, NIL, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word_add(m, word(4)))), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, NIL, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_prev_neighbour_test`

```
∀ (ys qs zs ts : List[W32]) (m p n d top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. !(p == word(0))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(APPEND(qs, [p]), CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(APPEND(qs, [p]), CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               APPEND(qs, [p]),
               m,
               zs,
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev_test(d, pop, push),
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(wasm_bool(!tlsf_used(hd(p)))))],
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word_add(m, word(4)))), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               APPEND(qs, [p]),
               m,
               zs,
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)))
```

```
∀ (d m r hm hr top : W32) (pop push : Num) (l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if

then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hm)),
          star(
            pts(val(r), serialise_i32(hr)),
            star(pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(top)), fp(gs, ms))))),
    tlsf_free_prev_write(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(hm))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(
            val(m),
            serialise_i32(
              word_or(
                word_and(
                  word_or(
                    word_and(hm, word(3)),
                    word_add(
                      word_add(
                        word_and(hm, word(4294967292)),
                        word_and(hr, word(4294967292))),
                      word(8))),
                  word(4294967293)),
                word(2)))),
          star(
            pts(val(r), serialise_i32(hr)),
            star(pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(m)), fp(gs, ms))))))
```

example `tlsf_free_pop_invoke_g`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)
  (lc0 : List[Value]) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (us vs : List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  2. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    tlsf_proved_spec(
      sfunc_ind(inst, pop),
      tlsf_pop_code(d),
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      1,
      0),
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      false,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
    [Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(
                tlsf_bins(
                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
                fp(gs, ms)))))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pop_invoke`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m hd fl : W32) (pop : Num)
  (lc0 : List[Value]) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (us vs : List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
  3. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd), tlsf_slot_of(hd)) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))),
    [Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(tlsf_prev(us))),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(tlsf_hd(vs))),
              star(
                tlsf_bins(
                  d,
                  tlsf_pop_fl(hd, fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_sb(hd, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
                  tlsf_pop_bl(hd, bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
                fp(gs, ms)))))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_push_invoke`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m hd fl pf0 nf0 : W32)
  (push : Num) (lc0 : List[Value]) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. !(m == word(0))
  3. 16 <= val(tlsf_size(hd))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            pts(val(word_add(m, word(8))), serialise_i32(pf0)),
            star(
              pts(val(word_add(m, word(12))), serialise_i32(nf0)),
              star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp(gs, ms)))))),
    [Call(push)],
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) =>
        star(
          pts(val(m), serialise_i32(hd)),
          star(
            tlsf_bins(
              d,
              tlsf_push_fl(hd, fl),
              tlsf_push_sb(hd, sb),
              tlsf_push_bl(hd, m, bl)),
            fp(gs, ms)))),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_begin`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp(gs, ms)),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_clear(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               zs,
               tlsf_release(hd, m),
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pop_prev_pair`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m r top fl : W32)
  (pop lim : Num) (ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (lc0 : List[Value])
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, r)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  5. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), tlsf_mark(hd, r), bl)
  6. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               APPEND(ys, [m]),
               r,
               zs,
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    [Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pair(
               d,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               r,
               zs,
               hd,
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(m), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(m), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_pop_next_pair`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m r top fl : W32)
  (pop lim : Num) (ys zs us vs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (lc0 : List[Value])
  (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  5. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, zs))), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  6. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(r)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(r))) == APPEND(us, CONS(r, vs))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               CONS(r, zs),
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    [Call(pop)],
    st(
      NIL,
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pair(
               d,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               r,
               zs,
               hd,
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(r), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(r), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(r), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_close`

```
∀ (ys zs : List[W32]) (d top m r fl : W32) (lim : Num) (hd nh : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : HProp) (h : Heap)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), nh, m)
  2. !tlsf_used(nh(m))
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(nh, m), bl)
  4. val(r) == val(m) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))
  5. val(word_add(r, word(4))) == val(r) + 4
  6. val(tlsf_size(nh(m))) == val(tlsf_size(hd(m))) + 8 + val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))
  7. ∀ x : W32, MEM(x, APPEND(ys, zs)) → nh(x) == hd(x)
  8. star(
       pts(val(tlsf_top_cell(d)), serialise_i32(top)),
       star(
         tlsf_regions(word(0), ys, hd),
         star(
           pts(val(m), serialise_i32(nh(m))),
           star(
             pts(
               val(word_add(m, word(4))),
               serialise_i32(tlsf_end(word(0), ys))),
             star(
               tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))),
               star(
                 pts(val(r), serialise_i32(hd(r))),
                 star(
                   pts(val(word_add(r, word(4))), serialise_i32(m)),
                   star(
                     tlsf_bytes(val(r) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(r)))),
                     star(
                       tlsf_regions(m, zs, hd),
                       star(tlsf_bins(d, fl, sb, bl), fp))))))))),
       h)
then
  star(tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, ys, m, zs, nh, fl, sb, bl), fp, h)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_prev_last`

```
∀ (ys ts : List[W32]) (n m r d top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])
  (l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), hd, r)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m, r]), tlsf_mark(tlsf_mark(hd, r), m), bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pair(d, top, ys, m, r, NIL, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev_write(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(hd(m)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               m,
               ys,
               m,
               NIL,
               tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_prev_interior`

```
∀ (ys ts : List[W32]) (n m r d top fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32])

       d,
       lim,
       top,
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, CONS(n, ts)))),
       hd,
       r)
  2. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, CONS(n, ts)))),
       tlsf_mark(tlsf_mark(hd, r), m),
       bl)
then
  sl_triple(
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pair(d, top, ys, m, r, CONS(n, ts), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev_write(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(hd(m)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               CONS(n, ts),
               tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_prev_last_body`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (ys ts us vs : List[W32]) (n m r d top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, [m, r]), hd, r)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  5. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, [m, r]), tlsf_mark(hd, r), bl)
  6. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(m)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(m))) == APPEND(us, CONS(m, vs))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(r)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               APPEND(ys, [m]),
               r,
               NIL,
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev_body(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(hd(m)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               m,
               ys,
               m,
               NIL,
               tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(m), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(m), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(m), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_merge_next_interior_body`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (ys ts us vs : List[W32]) (n m r d top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(
       d,
       lim,
       top,
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, CONS(n, ts)))),
       hd,
       m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. !tlsf_used(hd(r))
  5. tlsf_bins_match(
       APPEND(ys, CONS(m, CONS(r, CONS(n, ts)))),
       tlsf_mark(hd, m),
       bl)
  6. bl(tlsf_level_of(hd(r)), tlsf_slot_of(hd(r))) == APPEND(us, CONS(r, vs))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               CONS(r, CONS(n, ts)),
               hd,
               fl,
               sb,
               bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_next_body(d, pop, push),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(r)), V_num(ConstInt32(hd(m)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               ys,
               m,
               CONS(n, ts),
               tlsf_merge_headers(hd, m, r),
               tlsf_pop_fl(hd(r), fl, sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_sb(hd(r), sb, APPEND(us, vs)),
               tlsf_pop_bl(hd(r), bl, APPEND(us, vs))),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_start`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (ys zs : List[W32]) (m d top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  2. tlsf_used(hd(m))
  3. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, bl)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fl, sb, bl),
             star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp(gs, ms)),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_clear(d, pop, push),
    tlsf_free_work(d, lim, m, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, fp, false, false),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_publish_call`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m top fl : W32)
  (push lim : Num) (ys zs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (lc0 : List[Value]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  5. tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
  6. tlsf_free_right(zs, hd)
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, ys, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    [Call(push)],
    st(
      [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      lc0,
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(
               d,
               lim,
               top,
               APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)),
               hd,
               tlsf_push_fl(hd(m), fl),
               tlsf_push_sb(hd(m), sb),
               tlsf_push_bl(hd(m), m, bl)),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    NIL)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_prev_boundary_path`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (m0 m p n d top fl : W32) (xs0 qs zs ts : List[W32])
  (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (pop push lim : Num) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (l1 l2 : Value) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(NIL, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(NIL, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  5. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, hd)
  6. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(NIL, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
then
  sl_triple_g(

    fs,
    tabs,

        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, NIL, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_prev(d, pop, push),
    tlsf_free_work(d, lim, m0, xs0, hd0, fp, true, false),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_publish_arg`

```
∀ (m : W32) (l1 l2 : Value) (R : GProp),
  sl_triple(
    st(NIL, [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2], R),
    [Local_get(0)],
    st([V_num(ConstInt32(m))], [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2], R))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_finish_focused`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (d m0 top0 fl0 top m fl : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (xs0 ys zs : List[W32]) (hd0 hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb0 sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl0 bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp) (l1 l2 : Value)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  2. tlsf_free_geom(d, lim, top, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd, m)
  3. !tlsf_used(hd(m))
  4. tlsf_bins_match(APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), tlsf_mark(hd, m), bl)
  5. tlsf_free_covers(m0, hd0, m, hd)
  6. tlsf_free_live(xs0, hd0, m0, APPEND(ys, CONS(m, zs)), hd)
  7. tlsf_free_left(ys, hd)
  8. tlsf_free_right(zs, hd)
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), l1, l2],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_free_pending(d, lim, top, ys, m, zs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             fp(gs, ms),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_publish(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(
        d,
        m0,
        lim,
        top0,
        xs0,
        hd0,
        fl0,
        sb0,
        bl0,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_finish`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (rp : SAssn)
  (labels : List[SAssn]) (d m0 top0 fl0 : W32) (pop push lim : Num)
  (xs0 : List[W32]) (hd0 : (W32) -> W32) (sb0 : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl0 : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    rp,
    tlsf_free_work(d, lim, m0, xs0, hd0, fp, true, true),
    tlsf_free_publish(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(
        d,
        m0,
        lim,
        top0,
        xs0,
        hd0,
        fl0,
        sb0,
        bl0,
        fp,
        vs,
        gs,
        ms,
        h),
    labels)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_body_focused`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
  3. tlsf_physical_ok(d, lim, top, xs, hd)
  4. tlsf_bins_match(xs, hd, bl)
  5. MEM(m, xs)
  6. tlsf_used(hd(m))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h),
    st(
      NIL,
      [V_num(ConstInt32(m)), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))),
       V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))],
      (gs : List[Value]) => (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        ms == lim
        && star(
             tlsf_large(d, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl),
             star(tlsf_bytes(val(m) + 8, val(tlsf_size(hd(m)))), fp(gs, ms)),
             h)),
    tlsf_free_code(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h),
    [
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h)])
```

lemma `tlsf_free_body`

```
∀ (G : Specs) (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m top fl : W32)
  (pop push lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp) (args : List[Value])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_g(
    G,
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      vs == NIL
      && lc == APPEND(
           args,
           [V_num(ConstInt32(word(0))), V_num(ConstInt32(word(0)))])
      && tlsf_free_pre(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, args, gs, ms, h),
    tlsf_free_code(d, pop, push),
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h),
    [
      (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
      (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
        tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp, vs, gs, ms, h)])
```

definition `tlsf_free_spec`

```
tlsf_free_spec(
  (inst : Inst),
  (d : W32),
  (pop : Num),
  (push : Num),
  (free_idx : Num),
  (a : Num),
  (P : FProp),
  (Q : FProp)) == (
  a == sfunc_ind(inst, free_idx)
  && (
       ∃ m : W32,
         ∃ lim : Num,
           ∃ top : W32,
             ∃ xs : List[W32],
               ∃ hd : (W32) -> W32,
                 ∃ fl : W32,
                   ∃ sb : (Num) -> W32,
                     ∃ bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32],
                       ∃ fp : GProp,
                         P == tlsf_free_pre(
                           d,
                           m,
                           lim,
                           top,
                           xs,
                           hd,
                           fl,
                           sb,
                           bl,
                           fp)
                         && Q == tlsf_free_post(
                              d,
                              m,
                              lim,
                              top,
                              xs,
                              hd,
                              fl,
                              sb,
                              bl,
                              fp)))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_spec_call`

```
∀ (inst : Inst) (d m top fl : W32) (pop push free_idx lim : Num)
  (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32) (sb : (Num) -> W32)
  (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp),
  tlsf_free_spec(
    inst,
    d,
    pop,
    push,
    free_idx,
    sfunc_ind(inst, free_idx),
    tlsf_free_pre(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp),
    tlsf_free_post(d, m, lim, top, xs, hd, fl, sb, bl, fp))
```

lemma `tlsf_free_specs`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d : W32)
  (pop push free_idx k : Num)
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, free_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_free_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  specs_ok(k, tlsf_free_spec(inst, d, pop, push, free_idx), fs, tabs, inst)
```

lemma `tlsf_free_all`

```
∀ (fs : List[Cl]) (tabs : Tabs) (inst : Inst) (d m top fl : W32)
  (pop push free_idx lim : Num) (xs : List[W32]) (hd : (W32) -> W32)
  (sb : (Num) -> W32) (bl : (Num, Num) -> List[W32]) (fp : GProp)
  (vs0 lc0 : List[Value])
if
  1. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, free_idx), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_free_code(d, pop, push))
  2. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, pop), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], NIL),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_pop_code(d))
  3. EL(sfunc_ind(inst, push), fs) == Func_native(
       inst,
       Tf([T_num(T_i32)], [T_num(T_i32)]),
       [T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32), T_num(T_i32)],
       tlsf_push_code(d))
then
  sl_triple_m(
    fs,
    tabs,
    inst,
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ args : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, args)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_free_pre(
             d,
             m,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             fp,
             args,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    [Call(free_idx)],
    (vs : List[Value]) => (lc : List[Value]) => (gs : List[Value]) =>
    (ms : Num) => (h : Heap) =>
      ∃ res : List[Value],
        vs == APPEND(vs0, res)
        && lc == lc0
        && tlsf_free_post(
             d,
             m,
             lim,
             top,
             xs,
             hd,
             fl,
             sb,
             bl,
             fp,
             res,
             gs,
             ms,
             h),
    NIL)
```
